# Project State

## Project Reference

See: .planning/PROJECT.md (updated 2026-08-05)

**Core value:** A component-library author can write one `.rozie` file and ship working, idiomatic React + Vue + Svelte + Angular consumers from it — eliminating the manual cross-framework wrapper work that today dominates the maintenance budget of cross-framework UI libraries.
**Current focus:** Phase 87 — @rozie-ui/data-table horizontal (column) virtualization + content-driven auto-measure

**RESUME ANCHOR (2026-08-29 latest) — quick-260829-8lz CLOSED: React routes mount-frozen `$computed` reads through a live ref; premise re-aimed from a stale helper-class brief to the actually-open computed class.**
The brief's premise ("a top-level `<script>` helper consumed by a `$onMount`-registered effect is
frozen") was measured HALF WRONG: that helper class was already CLOSED on 2026-08-04 by `9acd77378`
(quick 260803-w7b seam 3, extended by 260806-w00 seam 4), and the `FlowCanvas.rozie` comment the brief
quoted (`982274e8c`, 2026-06-11) predates that fix by 8 weeks — its "ZERO emitter change" line is stale.
The actually-open class is `ir.computed`: a `$computed` lowers to a plain `useMemo` destructured const,
a `$onMount` hook lowers to a `[]`-dep `useEffect` by contract (mount-once, never re-created), so a
long-lived callback reading the computed bare captures the FIRST render's value forever even though the
`useMemo` recomputes on every dependency change. `emitScript.ts` had explicitly excluded `ir.computed`
from the mount-scope live-ref family with a rationale ("a fresh closure already sees the recomputed
value") that only holds for a closure React RE-CREATES, not a mount-once one. Fixed via a new
`mountReactiveComputedNames` discovery set (collision-guarded like `mountReadablePropNames`) routed into
its OWN `actuallyRewrittenComputed` set (never merged into `actuallyRewrittenModelProps`) with a
DEFERRED section 5e-bis emitted after the `ir.computed` `useMemo` loop — avoiding a temporal-dead-zone
`ReferenceError` a naive merge would hit (5b-bis, which the model-prop set drives, runs BEFORE that
loop). The mount effect's `exhaustive-deps` disable directive drops automatically once indirected (no
dep-filter change needed — verified empirically). Same-session Rule-1 fix: while proving the shadow
control, found that neither `rewriteWatchedPropReads` nor `findRefsInBody` actually consulted
`path.scope.getBinding(name)` despite both already traversing scope-aware synthetic ASTs — a
locally-shadowed state/model-prop/computed name inside a mount body was silently mis-rewritten to read
the OUTER binding. Fixed with the same shadow test already used by the sibling helper-rewrite pass
(D-11); zero corpus blast radius (full 588-test `@rozie/target-react` suite unchanged). RED-first proof
at two layers (emitter fixture: 2/6 red, 4 controls green pre-fix including a regression pin for the
now-closed helper class; a mounted `@testing-library/react` behavioral probe: failed pre-fix, "invoke"
observed the frozen value) — both verbatim outputs recorded in the quick task's SUMMARY. Corpus census
(12 `.rozie` files pairing `$computed` with `$onMount`, post-lower with `.rzts` partials correctly
resolved): ZERO shipped sites read a computed bare from a mount body — `DataTable.table`/
`refreshRowModel` are deliberately plain `let`s (imperative-pull, independent reasons to stay so),
`FlowCanvas.portTypeOf` and `PdfViewer`'s `$watch` hand-off are the ALREADY-CLOSED helper class and are
candidates for future simplification, left untouched. Whole-repo gates: build 243/243, test 140/140
(`@rozie/target-react` 588, `strict-conformance` 18, `dist-parity` 1049 — zero drift), dist-parity
re-bootstrap zero diff, typecheck 315/315, `fixturesLintCheck`/`exhaustive-deps-gate` 6/6 clean. Full
Docker VR union: 2272 passed, 7 skipped, 1 unrelated flake (retried clean, `data-table-dropins[lit]`) in
20.8m; the 260828-uyn fence cells (`chart.spec.ts` `setDatasetVisibility`, `chart-coverage.spec.ts`
`updateMode`) stayed green across all 6 targets. No `@rozie-ui/*-react` leaf moved (0-site census
confirmed by 0 leaf diffs). Changeset added (patch: `@rozie/core` only — no leaf bump needed). 2 commits
on `main` (`9c4dcc337`..`e9aafab95`), NOT pushed. SUMMARY at
`.planning/quick/260829-8lz-react-top-level-helper-consumed-by-an-ef/260829-8lz-SUMMARY.md`.
NEXT: Dan's push decision (joins the standing unpushed batch).

**RESUME ANCHOR (2026-08-29) — quick-260828-uyn CLOSED: React stabilizes pure object/array literal consts with `useMemo(…, [])`.**

**RESUME ANCHOR (2026-07-16) — combobox-virtual-reactivity phase CLOSED (all 3 tasks executed autonomously, no checkpoint, gates green).**
Named standalone phase (combobox-keepopen precedent — not a ROADMAP entry). Made `@rozie-ui/combobox`'s
`virtual` prop **live-flippable at runtime** on all 6 targets — previously the TanStack virtualizer was

full `rete-flow-*` suite (126/126, zero flaky both times) plus dist-parity zero-diff (no

**GAP-CLOSURE 45-07 (2026-06-13, WR-02/WR-06) — recursive proxy-safe Vue `$clone` SHIPPED (DIRECTION A FIX).**
Task-1 probe (`tests/visual-regression/scripts/clone-nested-probe.mjs`, commit `7ba4eb22`) PROVED the Vue
`structuredClone(toRaw(x))` lowering THREW one level deep — a single top-level `toRaw` leaves a NESTED
INDEPENDENT `reactive()`/`ref()` member (or array of reactive items, or FlowCanvas `$clone({d:src.data}).d`)
live, and `structuredClone` rejects it; Svelte's `$state.snapshot` recursively de-proxies and never shared
the hole. User chose FIX. New `@rozie/runtime-vue` export `rozieDeepClone(x) = structuredClone(deepToRaw(x))`
(WeakMap-guarded recursive de-proxy walk incl Map keys/values + Set members; preserves Date/Map/Set/cycles;
still throws on functions = preserved). Vue `$clone` now emits `rozieDeepClone(x)` via the runtime-vue
ScriptInjection dedupe path; `toRaw` dropped (usesToRaw→usesDeepClone). FlowCanvas:761 simplified
`$clone({d:src.data}).d`→`$clone(src.data)`; rete-vue leaf gained `@rozie/runtime-vue` dep (first leaf to
use `$clone` — its omission broke @rozie/docs build). dist-parity reblessed: drift confined to the Vue
CloneProbe leg ONLY (other 5 targets ZERO drift), 827/827. rozieDeepClone 11/11, probe SAFE×9 (HAZARD
ELIMINATED), core 1541, target-vue 330, runtime-vue 42, typecheck 144/144, **rete VR undo+reconnect 12/12**
in pinned container. Commits `5890f573` (helper+emitter+tests) / `e3de5c46` (rebless+dogfood+leaves+probe)
UNPUSHED. Vue now at parity with Svelte's recursive snapshot — Phase 45's universal claim holds at every depth.

Zero emitter change preserved (a render failure answered with an authoring-pattern change, NOT an emitter edit — the correct SCOPE-FENCE response). **Plan 19-01 (Wave 1 — `<listeners>` parser front-end swap) closed 2026-05-31.** `parseListeners.ts` is rewritten from a Babel object-literal walk to an htmlparser2 `<listener>` element-walk mirroring `parseTemplate`. Per `<listener>`: `:target` window/document/(omitted→`$el`) resolves (ROZ114 on anything else incl `$refs.x`, deferred — restriction enforced at the parser layer, `decodeTarget` untouched); N `@event` attrs fan out to N `ListenerEntry` sharing target + `r-if`; `r-if`→synthesized `when`; ROZ015 on zero-`@event`; self-close == paired; stray/unterminated markup yields a clean diagnostic; never throws (D-08). **The Req-6 synthesis bridge (load-bearing):** each entry's `value` is a synthesized `{when?,handler}` ObjectExpression, so `lowerListeners`/`buildDepGraph`/`unknownRefValidator`/`decodeTarget`/`resolveModifierPipeline`/all six emitters run UNCHANGED — the synthesized `when` StringLiteral `.start` = real `r-if` value offset − 1 so unknownRefValidator's `+1` quote-skip re-parse lands byte-accurately (tested this wave, not deferred). The `@event` parts go through a NEW shared `parseEventAttrName` helper (`parseEventAttr.ts`) that `parseTemplate` also now calls (D-20 structural; parseTemplate behavior byte-unchanged). Added ROZ015/ROZ114/ROZ206; retired ROZ010/011/012/013 object-walk EMISSION from parseListeners while keeping all DEFINITIONS (ROZ010/011 shared with parseProps/data/components). New `listenerElementValidator` (ROZ206, case-folded) wired final in `analyzeAST`. `ListenerEntry.rawKey/rawKeyLoc` now optional. Commits `c543ca9d` (parseEventAttr extraction), `2350c37e` (parseListeners element-walk + codes), `205c45e3` (listenerElementValidator). NOT pushed. NO emitter/lowerListeners/decodeTarget/normalize/buildDepGraph/unknownRefValidator file touched (plan diff = exactly the 10 files_modified). `@rozie/core` typecheck clean; the 3 new/rewritten suites GREEN (parseEventAttr 5/5, parseListeners 16/16, listenerElementValidator 4/4); pre-existing parseTemplate + source-location template tests stay green (behavior-preserving extraction).

**Plan 18-02 (Wave 2 — per-target `$model` lowering on React/Solid/Lit) closed 2026-05-31.** The three JSX-family targets now lower the producer-side two-way-write sigil `$model.X` through their EXISTING per-target setter/getter, at every guard site across BOTH `rewriteScript.ts` (`<script>` writes + reads) AND `rewriteTemplateExpression.ts` (`@event` handler writes + bindings). Mechanism (the load-bearing decision): instead of widening each `obj.name === '$props'` guard inline, each rewrite file runs a single `normalizeModelAccessor` pre-pass that renames the object Identifier of every non-computed Member/OptionalMember expression `$model`→`$props` before the main traversal — correct because `$model` is model-only by contract (Wave 1 rejected non-model/non-existent `$model.X` via ROZ205/ROZ113 pre-lowering) and always a member-expression object (D-03). This also normalizes RHS self-reads, so `$model.x = $model.x + 1` yields the same functional-updater as the `$props` form (a per-site widen would have emitted a stale-closure capture and broken byte-identity). Writes → React `setValue(prev => …)` / Solid `setValue(…)` createControllableSignal / Lit `_valueControllable.write(…)`; reads → React bare `value` / Solid `value()` / Lit `this.value`. Per-target `model-sigil.test.ts` (React `tests/`, Lit `tests/`, Solid `src/__tests__/` — Solid's vitest include is `src/**` only) assert byte-identity vs the `$props` twin fixture + script/template write + read + "no literal `$model.` survives". Gates: target-solid 222/222, target-lit 292/292, target-react 342/343 (the 1 = the EXPECTED ROZ204 carry-forward below — `examples/Counter.rozie` still writes `$props.value`, empty `.d.ts`; resolves in Wave 3). No existing snapshot drift. Commits `7f687eac` (React), `c736192a` (Solid+Lit). NOT pushed. Vue/Svelte/Angular `$model` lowering is a sibling Wave-2 plan; Wave 3 (18-03/04) migrates examples + reblesses the 4 gate families.

**Plan 16-03 ($restoreFocus sigil) closed 2026-05-26.** New author-surface sigil `$restoreFocus(selector, idx)` ships end-to-end. Per-target lowering: React/Vue/Angular emit `void 0` (keyed reconcilers MOVE existing DOM, focus survives); Svelte/Solid emit `queueMicrotask(() => $el.querySelectorAll(sel)?.[idx]).focus())` (synthesised `$el` flows through existing Identifier handler → `$refs.__rozieRoot` → target-native template-ref); Lit emits the same shape INLINE with `this.renderRoot` (NO runtime helper — RuntimeLitImport union unchanged per RESEARCH §Pitfall 7). Compile-time validator `validateRestoreFocus.ts` (1:1 mirror of validateClassSelector) wired into `lowerToIR` after validateClassSelector; emits ROZ975 (non-literal selector) and ROZ976 (wrong arity), priority arity-first, at most one diagnostic per call. Two-set sigil registration: RESERVED_SIGILS (ROZ202 collision guard) + STABLE_IDENTIFIERS (React dep-collector skip) — NOT MAGIC_ACCESSOR_NAMES per RESEARCH correction (callable like $classSelector, not member-accessor cluster like $attrs). Extended `lowerRootElementRef.ts` to detect `$restoreFocus(...)` calls in addition to free `$el` reads so Svelte/Solid synthesise `__rozieRoot` even when user source never explicitly reads `$el`. IntelliJ MAGIC_IDENTIFIERS + rozie-globals.d.ts + TextMate magic-identifier alternation: 16 → 17 entries in lockstep. Gates: turbo build 28/28, typecheck 47/47, target test suites all green (12 new restoreFocus snapshot tests pass + 7 validator regression tests), dist-parity 536/536 (no fixture drift — no existing fixture calls `$restoreFocus`), IntelliJ gradle test BUILD SUCCESSFUL.

Vue 3.4 D-02 documented as a known divergence (compiler-sfc blocks the workaround; Vue 3.5+ delivers natively via vuejs/core#11668 — installed Vue 3.5.33 passes). Identity-probe redesigned from cross-render to self-equality (`$props.e === $props.e` in same expression) to sidestep framework state-proxy wrapping. Template `String(...)` swapped for ternary because Angular templates don't expose `String`. Gates: turbo build 28/28, typecheck 47/47, test 47/47, dist-parity 536/536.

**Plan 14-06 (proving wave) closed 2026-05-22.** Substrate shipped clean: ThemedButton trio + RBindProbe fixtures byte-equal across CLI/Babel/unplugin × 6 targets × 4 entrypoints (96 outputs); IntelliJ + TextMate `$attrs` + object-form `r-bind` sync; structural smoke spec; VR matrix cells; vr.sh extended to super-tool (`-u`/`-s`/`-g`/`-b`/`-h` with rsync-back); DEBUG.md updated; Vue Linux baseline PNG committed; locator narrowing on the structural smoke; per-cell `test.fixme` gating with explicit Phase 14.1 hypothesis comments on the 5 non-Vue ThemedButtonConsumer cells.

**Substrate gates green:** turbo build 26/26, typecheck 47/47, test 46/46, dist-parity 512/512, textmate 11/11, IntelliJ gradle test BUILD SUCCESSFUL, vr.sh matrix + structural smoke pass-or-fixme on all 6 targets.

**Discovered render gap (carry-forward to Phase 14.1):** 5 per-target spreadBinding-emit regressions surfaced by the new local VR gate that the dist-parity byte-equality gate cannot catch (parity proves entrypoint equivalence, not render correctness). Full divergence catalog in `.planning/phases/14-cross-framework-attribute-fallthrough/14-06-SUMMARY.md § Cross-Target Divergence Catalog` — paths preserved to per-target diagnostic regen recipe. The 5 cells are quarantined in `matrix.spec.ts` PHASE_14_1_FOLLOWUP (NOT the looser KNOWN_CROSS_TARGET_DIVERGENCE set) + `themed-button.spec.ts` PHASE_14_1_FOLLOWUP_TARGETS. Phase 14.1 scope: react `style` string→object, svelte camel→kebab attr preservation, solid R6 class-merge for spreadBinding, angular style-merge replay, lit synthesis-gate widening for explicit-manual r-bind+inherit-attrs=false.

**vr.sh as the structural fix to `feedback_local_gate_mirrors_ci`:** without it the 5 render regressions ship undetected — dist-parity is necessary but insufficient. This is a load-bearing architectural win, not a debugging convenience. Memory `feedback_docker_clobbers_host_native_bindings` can be restated to point at vr.sh as the structural fix; memory `feedback_local_gate_mirrors_ci` can append vr.sh to the CI-mirror battery (orchestrator's call post-finalize).

**Plan 14-05 (Angular + Lit spreadBinding + synthesis un-gate) is complete.** All 6 targets now emit `spreadBinding` with their native idiom; `synthesizeAttrsFallthrough` is wired in `lowerToIR` so auto-fallthrough is on by default. The per-target `$attrs` lowering map (Vue passthrough / Svelte `__rozieAttrs` / React `attrs` IIFE / Solid splitProps rename / Angular `__rozieGetHostAttrs` / Lit `this.$attrs`) is locked.

**Plan 14-04 (Vue + Svelte spreadBinding) is complete.** Vue emits `v-bind=<obj>`; Svelte 5 emits `{...obj}` and uses `__rozieAttrs` rest binding (legacy `$$restProps` is illegal in runes mode).

**Plan 14-03 (React + Solid spreadBinding) is complete.** React emits JSX `{...attrs}` with an IIFE-scoped rest destructure; Solid uses `splitProps` with the rest renamed `attrs`.

**Plan 14-02 (r-bind lowering + fallthrough validator) is complete.** `r-bind="<expr>"` parses and lowers to a source-ordered name-less `spreadBinding`; the `r-bind:foo` colon form errors `ROZ969` at parse time; the new `validateAttrFallthrough` IR validator emits R8 `ROZ970` (multi-root error) + R9 `ROZ971` (double-apply warning), wired into `lowerToIR`. All 6 target emitters now handle the `spreadBinding` kind — Vue/React/Solid emit the real near-native spread (`v-bind`/`{...obj}`), Angular+Lit skip it (their bespoke `effect()`+Renderer2 / `rozieSpread` directive emit is Wave 3 / Plan 14-03). `turbo run typecheck` is now FULLY GREEN (47/47) — the Wave 0 `spreadBinding` exhaustiveness-red gate is resolved. Full `@rozie/core` suite: 1031/1031 (all 3 Wave 0 RED scaffolds green). **`synthesizeAttrsFallthrough` is implemented but intentionally NOT yet called** — synthesizing the `$attrs` spread by default emits a `$attrs` magic-accessor Identifier no target can lower until Plan 14-03 ships per-target `$attrs` lowering; synthesis + that lowering land together in 14-03 (see 14-02-SUMMARY § Deviations, Rule 4).

**Plan 14-01 (Wave 0 foundation) is complete.** The attribute-fallthrough type-and-registration substrate landed: the `spreadBinding` IR variant (name-less D-07 5th `AttributeBinding` member), `IRComponent.inheritAttrs` threaded through `lowerToIR`, `$attrs` registered across all three accessor sets, `<rozie inherit-attrs>` parsed through `BlockMap.rozie`, the ROZ969-ROZ971 diagnostic block, and four Wave 0 test scaffolds.

**Phase 10 is complete.** `<style lang="scss">` is supported end-to-end: a SCSS style block is compiled to plain CSS at compile time (dart-sass, before the PostCSS scoping pass), the compiled CSS is spliced into the emitter source string so all six target emitters need zero change, and the shipped output across all four entrypoints carries zero preprocessor dependency. The SCSS proving fixture `PortalListStyledScss` is the 12th dist-parity example; `pnpm --filter dist-parity test` is 392/392 green.

**All roadmapped phases are complete** — Phases 1–10 plus every decimal insertion (06.1–06.4, 07.1–07.5, 07.3.1, 07.3.2, 07.3.2.1, 08.1, 08.2). The compiler ships six targets end-to-end (React, Vue, Svelte, Angular, Solid, Lit); the IntelliJ plugin shipped through the 08.2 injection-first rebuild.

## Accumulated Context

### Decisions

Decisions are logged in PROJECT.md Key Decisions table.
Recent decisions affecting current work:

- [Phase 28]: Plan 28-04: VR determinism for an engine wrapper with a today/now concept requires pinning BOTH the view anchor (`initialDate`) AND the engine clock (`now`). `.fc-day-today` is driven by FullCalendar's `now` option (defaults to the live system clock) INDEPENDENTLY of `initialDate` — so pinning only the month leaves the today-highlight date-fragile, drifting the shared baseline red on every day but the capture day (the 2026-05-22 daily-flake class that pulled the original FullCalendar cell). Fix = `now: '2026-06-15'` in the demo `:options` (`0240d3a1` + `2bf15dc0`). Caught at the human-verify VISUAL sign-off gate, NOT by the green test run — concrete proof the visual sign-off is a real safety net, not a rubber stamp.
- [Phase 28]: Plan 28-04: ZERO rebless — a `@rozie-ui` WRAPPER `.rozie` surface change does not touch the emitter, so dist-parity (683/683), the `@rozie/target-*` snapshot suites, and the `@rozie/core` match-* cross-target snapshots correctly showed NO drift on the cold `test --force --continue`. No `pnpm --filter dist-parity bootstrap` run. The expected outcome, confirmed empirically.
- [Phase 28]: Plan 28-02 (depended on by 28-04): two OUT-OF-PLAN producer-side emitter fixes (both Rule-1, owner-approved, collision-gated, ZERO snapshot/parity churn) — a `nowIndicator` boolean prop and a `nowIndicator` portal-slot collided on the same emitted member name in Lit (`adf143df` + leaf `8f53976d`) and Svelte (`95653609` + leaf `5380b51f`); fixed by disambiguating the slot member (e.g. `nowIndicatorSlot`), firing ONLY on a portal-slot-name-vs-declared-prop collision. React/Vue/Angular/Solid immune by construction.
- [Phase 28]: Plan 28-04 follow-up FILED (owner "Approve, but file follow-up"): consumer-side prop/slot same-name collision — a consumer that both binds `:nowIndicator` AND fills `#nowIndicator` has the slot snippet SHADOW the prop binding. Same collision class 28-02 fixed producer-side, but at the consumer-binding layer; OUT of Phase 28 scope. Backlog: `.planning/todos/pending/consumer-prop-slot-name-collision.md` (cross-refs `adf143df`/`95653609`).

- [Phase 22]: Plan 22-06: angular cross-rozie red cell RESOLVED via TYPE-ONLY emitAngularTypes change — emit `export declare class <Name>` (NAMED + default). With a sidecar present, the cross-rozie shim's `export * from './X.rozie'` resolves to the sidecar; the named class lets `export *` re-export it, satisfying `import { X }` for BOTH type and value position (a `declare class` is value+type for tsc → works in Angular `imports: [X]`). CONTRADICTS 22-05 verified-fact-(c) ('named export declare class does NOT fix it') — re-tested empirically, the named export DOES fix the typecheck; fact (c) likely conflated typecheck with a runtime-value concern (handled by the disk-cache at bundle time). Runtime AOT path UNCHANGED.
- [Phase 22]: Plan 22-06: REVERTED dead-end — rewriting the cross-rozie shim emitter (transform.ts) to `export * from './X.rozie.js'` greened the typecheck with NO sidecar change, but regressed the angular e2e to 'JIT compiler unavailable' because `.rozie.js` bypasses the @analogjs vite-plugin-angular AOT transform (matches only `.rozie` → `.rozie.ts` virtual). The runtime shim specifier MUST stay `./X.rozie`; the typecheck fix belongs in the sidecar (type-only), not the shim.
- [Phase 22]: Plan 22-06: react/solid/lit ALSO need `allowArbitraryExtensions: true` (Rule-3 discovery — SPIKE-FINDINGS only tested vue/svelte/angular). Plain tsc 5.x with `moduleResolution: bundler` does NOT auto-enable it (TS6263 'allowArbitraryExtensions is not set' once the shadowing wildcard is removed). Only vue (vue-tsc) honors the sidecar under the bundler default. Wildcard demotion is conditional: delete when all `.rozie` imports are in-`src` sidecar'd (react/svelte/lit/angular); keep a `@deprecated` fallback for cross-root `examples/*.rozie` imports (vue/solid) — with the flag, a present sidecar takes precedence over the fallback (not type-lying for sidecar'd files).
- [Phase 22]: Plan 22-05 CHECKPOINT — Option A (orchestrator-approved, auto-mode): FINALIZE Plan-05 as-is. The whole-repo typecheck is red in EXACTLY one cell (angular-analogjs-demo, 4 cross-rozie cells TS2614); this is a KNOWN, DOCUMENTED Plan-06 hand-off, NOT a Plan-05 defect. Build + test gates whole-repo GREEN; only the angular typecheck cell is red. Plan-05's own deliverables (emitSidecar + buildStart wiring + CLI fallback + REQ-7) are complete and unit/build/test-green; the red cell is an integration concern Plan-06 (demo migration off the wildcard shim) is scoped to resolve.
- [Phase 22]: Plan 22-05 PLAN-06 ENTRY CONDITION (root cause of the angular red cell): with a `<Name>.d.rozie.ts` sidecar present and `allowArbitraryExtensions` OFF, TS resolves `./CardHeader.rozie` inside the Angular cross-rozie shim (`export * from './CardHeader.rozie'`) by stripping `.rozie` and picking `CardHeader.d.rozie.ts` (sidecar) over `CardHeader.rozie.ts` (disk-cache impl) → TS2614. Verified: (a) deleting sidecars restores GREEN angular typecheck; (b) SPIKE-FINDINGS only tested the DIRECT `import { SpikeProps } from './Spike.rozie'` form, never the cross-rozie `export *` shim path; (c) named `export declare class` sidecar does NOT fix it. Plan-06 must add `allowArbitraryExtensions:true` to angular-analogjs + svelte-vite (Vue does not need it) AND resolve the shim-vs-sidecar shadowing (declare module './X.rozie', explicit shim re-export from the disk-cache .rozie.ts, or another coordinated approach), then demote the wildcards.
- [Phase 22]: Plan 22-05: renderSidecar factored as a PURE shared function called by BOTH the unplugin emitSidecar AND the CLI build/watch fallback — single renderer, zero drift between the two emit paths (the explicit avoid of the build/watch shared-compile() drift class). Sidecar generation hangs off the SHARED unplugin buildStart hook (all six adapters), NOT a Vite-only API; handleHotUpdate sidecar re-emit is an additive Vite-only dev refresh. REQ-7 LOCKED GITIGNORED — *.d.rozie.ts already matched by .gitignore:29 *.rozie.ts; NO new rule.
- [Phase 08.3]: Plan 08.3-01: Co-located shape (a) — `RozieScriptDeclReference.kt` holds the reference class + companion walker + the provider class (single auditable unit). Mirrors `RoziePropsReference.kt` + `RozieMagicAccessReferenceProvider` (in `RozieJSReferenceContributor.kt`) co-location precedent. PATTERNS.md offered shape (a) or shape (b); chose (a) for DRY.
- [Phase 08.3]: Plan 08.3-01: Provider visibility is `internal class` NOT `private class` (deviation from PLAN.md text). Kotlin top-level `private` is FILE-scoped — the contributor in a sibling file cannot see a `private class` in `RozieScriptDeclReference.kt`. `internal` keeps the class out of the plugin's public Kotlin surface while permitting cross-file registration; module-private is the right tool for a cross-file-but-not-cross-module provider class.
- [Phase 08.3]: Plan 08.3-01: ES6ImportSpecifier name-leaf accessor is `it.referenceNameElement` (inherited from JSPsiReferenceElement) NOT `it.specifier` (no such property on the interface). PLAN.md / PATTERNS.md text was wrong about the IntelliJ Platform API; inspected the compiled `.class` to find the correct accessor. Walker arm: `(it.alias as? PsiElement) ?: it.referenceNameElement`. Same intent (precise leaf for Find-Usages / Rename cursor landing); correct API.
- [Phase 08.3]: Plan 08.3-01: No plugin.xml change. No build.gradle.kts change. The existing `<psi.referenceContributor language="JavaScript" implementation="js.rozie.intellij.references.RozieJSReferenceContributor"/>` plugin.xml entry already covers the new provider — Pitfall 3 hygiene preserved.
- [Phase 08.3]: Plan 08.3-02: Synthetic-ambient guard added in-band to Plan 01's `findScriptDeclByName` — `if (name.startsWith("$")) return null`. Without this, the new bare-ident walker steals `$props` / `$data` / etc. resolutions from the platform-native JS resolver (which would otherwise return the synthetic-ambient JSVariable directly per Plan 16 Strategy-B), breaking `RozieGlobalsLibraryTest`. The walker returns the JSVariable's `.nameIdentifier` leaf instead of the JSVariable itself. 1-line guard with inline justification preserves both the new bare-ident behavior AND the existing magic-identifier behavior. Caught only by full `./gradlew test`, not by the new test class's narrower gate.
- [Phase 08.3]: Plan 08.3-02: SPEC Req 8 (bail-safe) relaxed to accept EITHER null OR a name-matching recovered target. The JetBrains JS parser is more forgiving than SPEC assumed — it recovers `function fmt(x { return x }` into a damaged JSFunctionDeclaration with `.name == "fmt"`, and the walker returns its `.nameIdentifier`. This is BETTER UX than null (Ctrl-click still navigates to the broken decl); we keep the recovery behavior and relax the test assertion accordingly. No-crash invariant preserved.
- [Phase 08.3]: Plan 08.3-02: SPEC Req 3 (modifier-arg JS sub-injection inside `<listeners>` JSON-keys) and SPEC Req 7 (cross-injection Find-Usages) deferred-with-documented-test-shape. Req 3 needs a new sub-injector arm walking JSON-key string content; Req 7 needs a `FindUsagesProvider` extension on top of the PsiReference contract. Both are architectural follow-ups out of Plan 02's test-only scope. The test methods assert the bail-safe contract (no exception) with leading comment blocks describing the flip-when-shipped contract.
- [Phase 08.3]: Plan 08.3-02: Host-vs-injected `myFixture.file` walk-back pattern is mandatory for any test that needs to call `.text` on the original fixture content. When the `<caret>` marker sits inside a JS injection, `myFixture.file` returns the injected JS PsiFile (its `.text` is the rozie-globals.d.ts prefix + the injected block-body fragment), NOT the host RozieFile. Always: `val hostFile = InjectedLanguageManager.getInstance(project).getTopLevelFile(myFixture.file) ?: myFixture.file` before searching fixture text. Plan 17 precedent; codified here for future test plans.
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-03: $restoreFocus registers in RESERVED_SIGILS + STABLE_IDENTIFIERS only — NOT MAGIC_ACCESSOR_NAMES. CONTEXT D-07 said all three, but RESEARCH §Three-Set Sigil Registration Pattern corrected this based on $classSelector precedent (callable; STABLE_IDENTIFIERS only). MAGIC_ACCESSOR_NAMES is for member-accessor clusters ($props.X, $attrs.Y) — $restoreFocus is a CallExpression
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-03: Lit lowers $restoreFocus INLINE — no runtime helper. RuntimeLitImport union unchanged. Per RESEARCH §Pitfall 7: $reconcileAfterDomMutation needs a runtime helper because lit-html's repeat cache requires render(nothing, …); $restoreFocus is pure DOM API (queueMicrotask + querySelectorAll + focus())
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-03: Extended lowerRootElementRef.ts to detect $restoreFocus(...) calls in addition to free $el reads. Without this, user source that uses $restoreFocus but never explicitly reads $el would emit a synthesised $el identifier that fails to resolve (no __rozieRoot ref synthesised). Target-agnostic — Lit uses this.renderRoot directly; React/Vue/Angular lower to void 0; only Svelte/Solid actually consume the synthesised ref
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-03: Validator per-call priority — ROZ976 (arity) wins over ROZ975 (literal). Mirrors validateClassSelector's at-most-one-diagnostic-per-call pattern. Test `$restoreFocus($data.cls)` (both wrong arity AND non-literal) emits exactly one ROZ976 diagnostic
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-03: Per-target snapshot regex must tolerate Babel's `?.[idx])` optional-computed-member emit shape. The arrow body emits as `querySelectorAll(sel)?.[idx]).focus()` — optional access on the NodeList result, then plain .focus() — NOT `[idx]?.focus()`. Initial test regex was wrong and got fixed (Rule 1)
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-03: Svelte $el resolution mechanism verified (RESEARCH Assumption A2 RESOLVED) — existing rewriteScript Identifier handler rewrites $el → $refs.__rozieRoot → bind:this={__rozieRoot}; our synthesised `$el` in the queueMicrotask arrow body flows through that visitor naturally. No template-ref synthesis fallback needed
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-01: Identity-probe redesigned from cross-render comparison (`$props.e === $data.__lastE` with onMount capture) to self-equality (`$props.e === $props.e` within the same expression). Cross-render assertion is contaminated by framework state-proxy wrapping (Svelte 5 `$state` wraps arrays on write; Vue's reactive proxy similarly) — would report "false" on those targets even when D-02 was satisfied. Self-equality genuinely tests "do two consecutive reads of $props.e in the same expression return the same reference?"
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-01: Angular template ternary `... ? 'true' : 'false'` instead of `String(...)` — Angular's template expression scope exposes only a few globals (`JSON`, `null`, `undefined`, `true`, `false`); `String` is silently undefined and the expression breaks the whole component CD cycle
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-01: React merged-props type uses `Omit<XProps, defaultedFields> & { ... }` not `XProps & { ... }`. Intersection narrows to the COMMON subtype (`string | undefined ∩ string | null = string`), stripping the `| null` widening; `Omit` cleanly removes the original field type so the override stands alone
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-01: Props-interface null-widening MUST be published on both inline emitPropsInterface.ts AND emitTypes.ts (.d.ts) — sub-component composition (`<Card onClose={props.onClose} />`) requires the SUB-component's Props interface to accept `| null` too; merged-props widening alone is insufficient
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-01: React useState(() => factory())[0] for once-per-instance lazy-init (NOT useRef — useRef stores its first arg verbatim, would cache the factory function rather than its invocation result). The setter is dropped because the cached value never mutates
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-01: Vue compiler-sfc blocks the D-02 cache workaround. `withDefaults`'s second argument MUST be a static object literal — references to module-scope vars (the natural cache target) raise "scope reference" compile errors. Vue 3.4 floor has factory-default re-invocation; Vue 3.5+ delivers D-02 natively via `instance.propsDefaults` (vuejs/core#11668); installed Vue 3.5.33 passes the runtime probe. Documented as a known Vue 3.4 limitation in vue/emit/emitScript.ts
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-01: Solid's `mergeProps({ ..., null-prop: null, ... })` flows correctly — mergeProps preserves null as distinct from undefined (right-hand `_props` wins ONLY when its key is defined; absent keys fall through to defaults). Factory invocation `(${raw})()` inside mergeProps is once-per-instance because mergeProps runs once at component setup
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-01: Svelte 5 `let { a = null } = $props()` destructure NATIVELY coerces undefined→null for primitive defaults (Svelte 5 destructure-default behavior) — empirical audit confirmed; no R1 emit change needed for primitive null on Svelte. D-02 factory caching IS new behavior on Svelte: module-init `let __defaultE = (factory)();` prelude + destructure references the cached name
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-01: Angular state-signal NullLiteral widening extends the existing `signal<any[]>(empty-array)` special-case in angular/emit/emitScript.ts:761 — `<data>{ observed: null }` would otherwise type as `Signal<null>` and reject downstream `observed.set({...})` writes. Same intent as `typeNeutralizeScript`'s `let editor = null` → `let editor: any = null` widening, but applied at class-field level (which typeNeutralizeScript doesn't reach)
- [Phase 16]: Plan 16-01: parity.test.ts has its own EXAMPLES array that's NOT auto-synced from bootstrap-fixtures.mjs — adding a fixture to bootstrap alone wouldn't actually run the +24 dist-parity cells. Extended parity.test.ts EXAMPLES 17 → 18 to include PropDefaultCoercion. Phase 15's three fixtures (ThemedButtonListenersManual, ThemedButtonAllManual, ROnProbe) + Phase 13's ClassSelectorProbe also missing from parity.test.ts EXAMPLES (separate audit item; out of scope for this plan)
- [Phase 14]: Plan 14-02: ROZ969 R_BIND_COLON_FORM emitted at PARSE level (ast/normalize.ts enrichAttr), NOT lowerTemplate as planned — the Wave 0 rbind-parse.test.ts scaffold observes it via parse() which does not run lowering; a lowerTemplate guard would be invisible + a both-places guard double-emits in compile() (which aggregates parse + lower diagnostics)
- [Phase 14]: Plan 14-02: synthesizeAttrsFallthrough is defined+correct but its lower.ts call is GATED OFF (`void synthesizeAttrsFallthrough`) — synthesizing the $attrs spread by default emits a $attrs magic-accessor Identifier no target's rewriteTemplateExpression can lower yet (Svelte rejects `{...$attrs}` — `$`-prefixed identifiers are illegal); synthesis + per-target $attrs lowering (React props rest / Svelte $$restProps / Solid splitProps / Angular+Lit bespoke) land together in Plan 14-03 (Rule 4 deviation)
- [Phase 14]: Plan 14-02: all 6 emitters get spreadBinding non-crash handling this wave — Vue `v-bind=` / React+Solid JSX `{...obj}` / Svelte `{...obj}` near-native; Angular+Lit skip (bespoke `effect()`+Renderer2 D-01 / rozieSpread directive D-02 is Wave 3 per 14-RESEARCH); D-20 synthetic fixtures + FullCalendar.rozie marked `inherit-attrs="false"` — genuinely multi-root, validateAttrFallthrough R8 ROZ970 correctly flags them
- [Phase 14]: Plan 14-01: spreadBinding is the name-less 5th AttributeBinding member (D-07) — its add reds the typecheck exhaustiveness gate in all 6 emitters (80 errors), the Wave 0 enforcement mechanism Phase 07.3 used for twoWayBinding; @rozie/core's own src/ files stay clean
- [Phase 14]: Plan 14-01: $attrs registered in all three accessor sets — RESERVED_SIGILS (ROZ202 shadowing), MAGIC_ACCESSOR_NAMES (member reads $attrs.x), STABLE_IDENTIFIERS (load-bearing: bare r-bind="$attrs" uses the bare-identifier form; without the STABLE entry the React dep collector leaks [$attrs] into a useEffect dep array → ReferenceError)
- [Phase 14]: Plan 14-01: attrsReservedSigil.test.ts placed in src/__tests__/ not the plan-stated src/semantic/validators/ — vitest's include glob is `src/**/__tests__/*.test.ts`; a .test.ts outside an __tests__ dir is silently uncollected ("No test files found")
- [Phase 14]: Plan 14-01: inheritAttrs threaded through lower.ts this plan (not deferred to a later wave) — Task 1 made IRComponent.inheritAttrs a REQUIRED field, breaking core's own construction site; lower.ts sets `ast.blocks.rozie?.inheritAttrs ?? true`; 10 IR `fixtures/ir/*.snap` reblessed for the +1 line `"inheritAttrs": true`
- [Phase 14]: Plan 14-01: <rozie inherit-attrs> parse — only the literal value `false` flips fallthrough off; absent attribute omits the BlockMap key (→ true default), present-without-value / garbage / "true" all → true, the safe default (threat T-14-01); savedInheritAttrsChunks uses null (absent) vs [] (present-without-value) to distinguish
- [Phase 14]: Plan 14-01: ROZ969 R_BIND_COLON_FORM / ROZ970 ATTR_FALLTHROUGH_MULTI_ROOT / ROZ971 ATTR_DOUBLE_APPLY allocated (ROZ968 was the verified prior highest); R1/R8/R9 Wave 0 test scaffolds are real failing tests (not it.todo), 3 RED gated on Plan 14-02
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-01: $classSelector diagnostic codes are ROZ965/ROZ966/ROZ967 (NOT ROZ964/965/966 as some planning text said) — ROZ964 was claimed by a Phase 12 fix (RMODEL_MODIFIER_NOT_APPLICABLE) committed since planning
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-02: ROZ965/966/967 (CLASS_SELECTOR_ARG_NOT_LITERAL / _UNKNOWN_CLASS / _INVALID_TOKEN) registered; validateClassSelector IR validator wired into lowerToIR — NOT compile.ts — because it needs only ir.styles (no resolver/cache) and lowerToIR is the single chokepoint compile() and @rozie/unplugin share (validateTwoWayBindings' 7-site pattern exists only because IT needs a resolver)
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-03: $classSelector lowered per target via a single `rewrite/lowerClassSelectorCall.ts` helper that BOTH rewriteScript.ts and rewriteTemplateExpression.ts call — eliminates the 2-hooks-drift risk (Pitfall 4); React → BinaryExpression `"." + styles.<class>` (computed member access `styles['x-y']` for hyphenated classes — `styles.x-y` is a TS parse error), Vue → `StringLiteral '.<class>'`
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-03: ClassSelectorProbe.rozie `<script>` comment rephrased to drop the bare `$classSelector` token — the 13-01 scaffold's `not.toContain('$classSelector')` assertion tripped on the preserved JS comment (not a live call); the lowering itself was correct
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-04: Svelte + Solid `$classSelector` lowered via the same per-target `rewrite/lowerClassSelectorCall.ts` shared-helper pattern as 13-03 — both lower to `StringLiteral '.<class>'` (the simple literal form; only React lowers to a runtime expr)
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-04: Solid `classSelector.test.ts` `:attr` assertion relaxed to `toMatch(/['"]\.panel['"]/)` — Solid's `rewriteTemplateExpression` generator forces `jsescOption.quotes='single'` (pre-existing Solid design — aligns with `_props.slots?.['x']` emit), so the `:attr` literal renders `'.panel'` while the `<script>` literal renders `".panel"`; quote style is incidental to lowering correctness
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-05: Angular + Lit `$classSelector` lowered via the same per-target `rewrite/lowerClassSelectorCall.ts` shared-helper pattern as 13-03/13-04 — both lower to `StringLiteral '.<class>'` (the simple literal form; only React lowers to a runtime expr). Completes all 12 rewrite-hook sites (6 targets × 2 files); no test-assertion relaxation needed — both Angular and Lit `rewriteTemplateExpression` generators emit double-quoted literals, so the 13-01 scaffold's `toContain('".panel"')` passed unmodified (unlike Solid)
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-06: Vue/Angular `:attr`-position `$classSelector` lowering must single-quote the lowered literal — a default double-quoted `".grip"` StringLiteral collides with the double-quoted `:attr="..."`/`[attr]="..."` template-binding wrapper, producing malformed `:handle="".grip""`. Fixed (Rule 1) via a `quoteStyle` param on `lowerClassSelectorCall` applied only on the `rewriteTemplateExpression` path (`extra.raw` single-quote); the `<script>` path stays double-quoted. Pre-existing 13-03 (Vue) / 13-05 (Angular) bug — the Wave-0 `toContain('".panel"')` matched the substring inside the malformed output and was too weak to catch it; relaxed to `toMatch(/['"]\.panel['"]/)` like Solid 13-04. React/Svelte/Solid/Lit unaffected (curly-brace bindings / BinaryExpression don't collide)
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-06: `@rozie/cli` `multi-target.test.ts` M2/M7 fail in this environment — pre-existing, out of scope: `runBuildMatrix` globs all of `examples/`, 12/412 compilations fail (8× ROZ085 SCSS fixtures — optional `sass` peer dep not installed; 4× ROZ960 `RModelCustom.rozie` `.phone` by-design absent from `createDefaultRegistry()`). All 12 files predate Phase 13-06; logged to `deferred-items.md`
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-07: D-01 synthetic-DragEvent technique locked via Vue spike — SortableJS v1.15 runs `supportPointer:true`, so it binds `pointerdown` (NOT `mousedown`) for `_onTapStart`; the down event must originate ON the `.grip` handle (`_onTapStart` enforces `options.handle`); SortableJS defers `_dragStarted` (which binds the document `dragover` listener) via `setTimeout(fn,0)` so the helper waits one macrotask between `dragstart` and the `dragover` burst; `dragend` is dispatched on the target row with `composed:true` (NOT document — a document-targeted dragend misses Lit's shadow-bound SortableJS listener). Full sequence: pointerdown+mousedown(grip) → dragstart(grip) → wait → dragenter+dragover×3(target lower-half) → drop(target) → dragend(target), one shared DataTransfer
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-07: `sortable-drag.spec.ts` drives the drag through Playwright `Locator`s (not `document.querySelector`) — locators pierce Lit's nested shadow DOM and substring `[class*="..."]` matchers survive React CSS-Modules class hashing; structural assertions only (no `toHaveScreenshot`) so it runs on macOS
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-07: lit `sortable-drag` cell carries a per-cell `test.fixme` (mirrors full-calendar.spec.ts per-cell gate) — the synthetic drag DOES drive a real SortableJS reorder on Lit and the bound `$data` syncs, but the DISPLAYED list reverts after `drop` because `SortableList.rozie`'s `onUpdate` `e.item.remove()`+`insertBefore` DOM-restore corrupts lit-html's part tree; NOT a `$classSelector` regression (the Lit cell's normal data path works); tracked as post-Phase-13 follow-up `rozie-lit-sortable-reconcile`
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-02: validateClassSelector scans four IR regions — <script> Program, template binding/interpolated/twoWayBinding expressions, listener when/handler, $computed bodies; per-call priority arity/literal (ROZ965) → token-shape (ROZ967) → unknown-class (ROZ966), at most one diagnostic per call
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-02: $classSelector added to STABLE_IDENTIFIERS (NOT RESERVED_SIGILS) — mirrors $snapshot; keeps the React dep collector from emitting [$classSelector] into a useEffect/useMemo dep array (Pitfall 2)
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-02: ComputedDecl carries `body` (Expression|BlockStatement), not `initializer` — the plan/RESEARCH text said `initializer` (that field is on StateDecl); validator scans computed.body, scanNode handles both shapes
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-01: all six per-target classSelector.test.ts placed uniformly in src/__tests__/ (matches plan frontmatter + solid's only convention); validateClassSelector.test.ts uses string-literal codes with a TODO until Plan 13-02 ships RozieErrorCode.CLASS_SELECTOR_* — referencing not-yet-existent symbols would block suite collection
- [Phase 13]: Plan 13-01: ClassSelectorProbe is the 14th dist-parity EXAMPLES fixture; single-file, exercises $classSelector in both <script> and a :attr binding so dist-parity covers both rewrite hooks

Also fixed a pre-existing shadow-rewrite gap in rewriteWatchedPropReads/findRefsInBody (neither consulted path.scope.getBinding before this quick, even though both already traverse scope-aware ASTs) — zero corpus blast radius, verified via the full 588-test target-react suite.

### Roadmap Evolution

- **Phase 86 added (2026-08-31)** — Combobox v1 gap cluster, promoted from ROADMAP 999.5 audit item 4 ("the least-complete leaf in the whole audit"). Chosen deliberately as feature work after a long emitter-bug run; the roadmap had no Phase 86 and every `todos/pending/` item is LOW-priority chore, which is *why* the work had drifted to bug-fixing. **Three real ❌s** from `docs/components/combobox-comparison.md`: multi-select/tags mode, floating-positioned popup (auto-flip/shift), free-text/creatable. **Two corrections to the audit entry, made at promotion time and written back into ROADMAP 999.5:** (a) its fourth listed gap "no option groups" is **stale** — groups shipped (`groups` prop + `role="group"` headings + `groupHeading` slot + `groupCap` "+N more"), as did opt-in `:virtual` windowing via `@tanstack/virtual-core`; (b) the floating-popup gap is **composition, not construction** — `@rozie-ui/popover` already wraps `@floating-ui/dom` and exposes `placement`/`offset`/`disableFlip`/`disableShift`/`arrow`, and 999.5 item 2 (Option A) is likewise stale since it **shipped as Phase 75**. Items 5 (toast, closed 2026-07-15) and 6 (rete chrome, closed as Phase 74) also marked closed in the same pass. **Primary technical risk identified up front:** `@rozie-ui/command-palette` already composes `@rozie-ui/combobox`, so putting popover under combobox makes a **2-level** published-leaf chain — Phase 75 only ever proved one level (data-table→popover, command-palette→combobox). De-risk with a nested-chain tracer ×6 BEFORE feature work; if `resolveManifestProducer`'s upward `node_modules` walk doesn't hold at depth 2, the floating-popup item changes shape entirely. **Constraint that must survive multi-select:** the single-`value`-model stance is load-bearing — a second model forfeits the clean Angular `ControlValueAccessor`, and a combobox is itself a form control. **Explicitly OUT of scope:** the two virtualization *combination* gaps (sticky group headers in the `:virtual` path, variable-height auto-measure) — those belong with 999.5 item 3, the virtualization round-out. NEXT: `/gsd-spec-phase 86`. See [[project_cross_family_components_composition_gap]], [[project_rozie_ui_distribution_model]], [[feedback_close_coverage_gaps_in_debut_wave]], [[feedback_emitter_owns_parity]].
- Phase 85 added: Volar-based .rozie language service across VS Code and IntelliJ (thin vertical slice; Kotlin retirement deferred to a later phase)
- Phase 84 added: FlowCanvas edge routing via ELK bendpoints — persist `connection.waypoints` from `edge.sections[].bendPoints` (quick 260826-h7k fixed the placement half; ELK's routes are already correct and we discard them).
- **Phase 82 SCOPE CORRECTED (2026-08-24, post-research)** — the Phase-82-added entry below is **wrong on three load-bearing points**; corrected here and in ROADMAP.md. (1) There is **no unfinished emitter path**: `@rozie/core` already ships a complete, tested attrs/listeners fallthrough system (`synthesizeAttrsFallthrough`/`synthesizeListenersFallthrough`, `ir/lowerers/lowerTemplate.ts`, wired `ir/lower.ts:456-457`) that works on every single-root component across all six targets, plus a **ROZ970 error** for multi-root + auto-inherit whose hint mandates `inherit-attrs="false"` + `r-bind="$attrs"`. (2) `rete` and `maplibre` **explicitly opted out and never finished the opt-out** — `FlowCanvas.rozie:90`, `NodeType.rozie`, `Port.rozie`, `MapLibre.rozie` all carry `inherit-attrs="false" inherit-listeners="false"` with the mandated `r-bind="$attrs"` never added. Incomplete opt-out in LEAF SOURCE, not an emitter bug. (3) **data-table is NOT affected** — all 12 `src/*.rozie` leaves are single-root; `DataTable.vue` emits exactly ONE root (`<div class="rozie-data-table-wrap" ref="__rozieRootRef">` wrapping everything, proven by tag-depth analysis: net-open-divs 0 across the whole template). The earlier "5 root nodes / the flagship is affected" figure was an artifact of a **bad line-indentation heuristic** (counting unindented lines as root siblings; emitted Vue does not indent nested children consistently). Real blast radius: **rete + maplibre, two families, flagship untouched.** The retracted "smoking gun" — Solid's `splitProps` binding `attrs` and never using it — is simply the expected consequence of `inherit-attrs="false"`, not evidence of an unfinished path. **Real gap:** `countRootElements` (duplicated in `ir/validateAttrFallthrough.ts:67` and a listeners-side twin that does NOT share code) counts any non-text sibling — including a `<slot>` invocation — as a root, so the "1 element + N slots" shape is misclassified multi-root and cannot auto-inherit despite having exactly one eligible element. **Second bug found:** a single-root template whose root is gated by `r-if` **silently drops attrs** — `countRootElements` returns 1 for a `TemplateConditional` so ROZ970 never fires, and synthesis no-ops on that shape → neither spread nor diagnostic (confirmed by the code's own comment, `validateAttrFallthrough.ts:60-64`). **METHOD LESSON: never count emitted-template roots by line indentation — use tag-depth analysis or `lowerToIR`.** Strategy A (leaf-only `r-bind="$attrs"`, zero core change, compile-verified on all 4 targets) vs Strategy B (generalize both `countRootElements` twins to ignore slot invocations, then drop the opt-outs) still to be decided. See [[feedback_emitter_owns_parity]], [[feedback_canonical_wins_dedup_verify_behavior]].

- **Phase 83 added (2026-08-24)** — `@rozie-ui/rete` FlowCanvas theming contract + visual/a11y cleanup + connection-drag affordance. Origin: an external audit of the shipped component (ISSUE-3/4/5/6) plus a follow-on rete visual pass run the same day. **ISSUE-4 was split OUT into Phase 82** — it is emitter-wide, not a rete bug. Confirmed at source: (**ISSUE-3**) the SFC `<style>` OS-dark block at `FlowCanvas.rozie:3939` has NO light-opt-out guard while `themes/base.css:161` guards the same palette with `:root:not(.light):not([data-theme="light"])`, making base.css's own documented "dark works with NO import, light opt-out honored" promise FALSE on the zero-import path, with no CSS-only consumer workaround (a wrapper descendant rule only TIES `(0,2,0)` and resolves on injection order; `color-scheme` cannot opt a subtree out of a `prefers-color-scheme` match). Dan chose **guard the five light-DOM targets and DOCUMENT the Lit gap** — Lit's copy lives in a shadow root (`FlowCanvas.ts:46` `static styles`) where `:root` matches nothing, and `:host-context()` was rejected as Chromium-only/silent-no-op in Firefox+Safari. (**ISSUE-5**) `.rozie-flow-node__body` is `min-width:0` only vs the title's `padding:.5rem .75rem`. (**ISSUE-6**) no drag-time feedback for an incompatible port; cheap because `portTypeOf` (`:2062`), `connectionpick`/`connectiondrop` (`:1349`/`:1368`) and the socket render emit carrying `{side,key,nodeId,element}` (`:1759`) all already exist — type-only hint, `canConnect` deliberately NOT invoked per-socket-per-pick. Visual audit added seven more: **A1** ZERO `:focus`/`:focus-visible` rules anywhere despite `tabindex="0"` on the canvas and three classes of interactive control (highest-severity audit finding); **A2** the socket edge-nudge is a hardcoded `-6px` decoupled from `--rozie-flow-socket-size`, and with no `box-sizing` the 2px border is uncounted so it is already ~2px off centre; **A3** 54 colour/size tokens but ZERO typography tokens (four hardcoded `system-ui` shorthands); **A4** dark-palette DRIFT already user-visible — `--rozie-flow-resize-handle-bg:#1e293b` is in the SFC OS-dark block but missing from BOTH base.css dark blocks, so a LIGHT-OS consumer on the `.dark` class strategy gets white NodeResizer handles on a dark node (Dan chose patch + **a drift test**, since the palette is hand-maintained in three places and this is the second drift symptom after ISSUE-3); **A5** dark never remaps `--rozie-flow-socket-ring` or the three `*-shadow` tokens, plus an untokenised shadow layer on `.is-selected`; **A6** scattered hardcoded chrome (LEAVE minimap `200×150` — coupled to `MINIMAP_W`/`MINIMAP_H`); **A7** `cursor:grab` never becomes `grabbing`. **Verified NOT a finding:** no `transition`/`animation` declarations exist at all, so `prefers-reduced-motion` has nothing to respect at the CSS layer. Open question for planning: VR-ing a TRANSIENT mid-drag state — existing baselines are static screenshots, likely needs a Playwright test that pauses mid-gesture. Gates: ONE batched `vr.sh` union run at phase END; several fixes legitimately require a rebless that must be diff-reviewed, not blind-accepted. NEXT: `/gsd-plan-phase 83`. See [[feedback_snapshot_tests_cement_bugs]], [[feedback_batch_vr_across_quick_series]], [[project_grid_active_cell_ring_state_driven]], [[project_theming_pages_generated]].

- **Phase 82 added (2026-08-24)** — Multi-root consumer attribute fallthrough in `@rozie/core`. A consumer's `class`/`style`/rest-attrs are SILENTLY DROPPED on four of six targets whenever a component renders multiple root nodes, so the per-instance inline-theming escape hatch (`:style="{'--rozie-flow-bg':'red'}"`) works on Angular and Lit and nowhere else. Surfaced as ISSUE-4 of a rete audit; **measured to be toolchain-wide and split into its own phase** rather than worked around in the rete template, per [[feedback_emitter_owns_parity]]. Per-target mechanism confirmed by reading emitted output: Vue renders a Fragment with no `inheritAttrs:false`/`$attrs` rebind; React returns `<>…</>` (`FlowCanvas.tsx:3863`) with no spread onto the root; Svelte destructures `$props()` with no rest (`:155`); **Solid calls `splitProps(_merged,[...])` at `:464` and binds `attrs` which is then NEVER REFERENCED again in the file** — the load-bearing evidence that this is an unfinished emitter path, not a design choice. Angular (`selector:'rozie-flow-canvas'`) and Lit (`@customElement` + `:host{display:contents}`) are correct BY CONSTRUCTION because each has a real host element that receives `class`/`style` natively and lets custom properties inherit down — so this is a cross-target PARITY gap, not a missing feature. Blast radius measured across the whole monorepo: three families ship multi-root emitted Vue today — **`data-table` (5 root nodes, the flagship traction bet)**, `maplibre` (2), `rete` (2); verification must cover data-table and maplibre consumers, not just rete. Likely hook: `packages/core/src/ir/lowerers/lowerRootElementRef.ts` already designates a primary root element for `$el`/`__rozieRoot` — reuse that selection rather than minting a second notion of "the root". Open design questions for planning: behavior when the designated root sits inside an `r-if`, and how a consumer `class` COMPOSES with an existing `:class` binding (merge, not replace). Gates are repo-wide: whole-repo `build --force` (core is inlined into plugin packages), cold `test --force --continue` with expected target-suite snapshot drift, `dist-parity bootstrap` rebless, entrypoint byte-equality, strict-TS consumer fixtures if `.d.ts` shifts, `ci-prepush`. NEXT: `/gsd-plan-phase 82`. See [[project_core_inlined_into_plugin_pkgs]], [[feedback_dist_parity_rebless_after_emitter_change]], [[feedback_target_suite_snapshots_drift_on_emitter_change]], [[project_data_table_traction_bet]], [[feedback_unplugin_resolveid_mediation]].
- **Phase 81 added (2026-08-23)** — Per-target rewrite of authoring notation inside emitted `@example` JSDoc. `buildPropJsdoc.ts:96` copies a prop's `docs.example` verbatim into all six target surfaces, so consumers reading `.d.ts` see `<FlowCanvas r-model:graph="graph" :validate-types="true" />` — notation no target but Vue accepts; `r-model` alone appears in **506 emitted leaf files across 26 families**. **Intake finding that widened the scope:** of the 41 distinct `docs.example` strings, 25 carry `r-model:` but **27 carry `:prop="expr"`**, several carry kebab attrs, one `@change=`, one `<template #body>` + `{{ }}` — so an r-model-only rewrite emits mixed notation that is still invalid on 5 of 6 targets. Dan chose **total per-target rewrite** (over r-model-only and over dropping `@example`), **Lit in-template form** (`.x=${e}` + `@x-change=${…}`, deliberately diverging from the imperative usage page), and **its own phase** (over `/gsd-quick`). Design: parse the example with the existing `parsers/parseTemplate.ts` — no regex — and render per-target consumer markup through ONE parameterised table threaded into the single builder, preserving the anti-drift one-builder precedent; any construct the renderer cannot cover is a **hard diagnostic**, never a silent passthrough. Prose (`"two-way `r-model`"` in descriptions) is an explicit non-goal. **Correction to the older deferral note: the claim that a per-target rewrite already exists in the usage-page generator is FALSE** — `gen-usage-pages.mjs` only collects snippets hand-authored per family per target in `packages/ui/*/scripts/readme.mjs`; no such transform exists anywhere in the repo, so this phase writes it. Gates: red-first in `build-prop-jsdoc.test.ts`, whole-repo `turbo run build --force` (core is inlined into plugin packages), cold `test --force --continue` for snapshot drift, `dist-parity bootstrap` rebless, strict-TS consumer fixtures, 26 family codegen regens, `scripts/ci-prepush.sh`. Comments-only — no VR expected. NEXT: `/gsd-spec-phase 81`. See [[project_docs_example_rmodel_jsdoc_leak]], [[feedback_emitter_owns_parity]], [[project_core_inlined_into_plugin_pkgs]].

- **Phase 80 added (2026-08-17)** — Angular record-path slot fills move from the `@ViewChild(static:true)` + `templates`-record consumer path onto a `[rozieSlot]` marker directive collected by `contentChildren`, shipped from a NEW `@rozie/runtime-angular` package (the first Angular runtime package; Angular was the only target without one). Driven by two **silent-wrong-render** bugs found by exploration the same day and confirmed with an Angular 21 TestBed probe: (1) a record-path fill inside `r-if`/`r-for` is silently dropped — `emitTemplateNode.ts:932` emits `static: true` unconditionally, a static query never resolves inside an embedded view and never recovers, so the producer's DEFAULT content renders instead of the consumer's fill; (2) two sibling producers collide — `dynIdx` restarts per component tag (`:871`) and the getter name is the constant `'templates'` (`:946`), so the second producer renders the FIRST one's fill body (probe: `A:L1A:L2`, correct `A:L1 B:L2`). Bug 2 was predicted verbatim as "Pitfall 4" in `07.3.2.1-RESEARCH.md:333-337` and never closed. Blast radius covers kebab `#cell-status` and `matchedFamily` fills too (the data-table pattern), but is **LATENT** — no shipped `@rozie-ui` leaf emits `__dynSlot_`. Ruled out: NG0100 is NOT a risk (`devModeEqual` exempts object-vs-object, `core.mjs:20639-20655`). Design decisions locked with Dan: record-path-only scope (~11 files vs ~78 if unified), `templates` input survives as a documented escape hatch, package charter is the directive ONLY (inlined helpers stay inlined), and the package builds with `ng-packagr` partial-Ivy rather than tsdown — a deliberate, documented deviation from CLAUDE.md's default, using the recipe already proven at `packages/ui/pagination/packages/angular`. The phase MUST also close the verification gap that let both bugs survive: there is no Angular runtime test harness (dist-parity is text-only; VR builds Angular with `vite build`, stripping `ngDevMode`).

- **Phase 79 added (2026-08-16)** — Producer-side dynamic slot names (`<slot :name="expr">`) across all six targets + the Lit `rozieSlots` record intake that closes the last ❌ in `docs/compatibility.md`. Scoped in brainstorming the same day; full design carried in the ROADMAP entry, ready for `/gsd-spec-phase 79`. Two findings drove it: (1) producer-side dynamic slot names are unsupported on **all six** targets, not just Lit — `lowerSlots.ts:172` reads only a static `name`, so a bound `:name` silently becomes a scope param via the `:param` collector at line 106; (2) that mis-routing is already producing **divergent per-target semantics** in four blessed regression fixtures (`loop-mustache-{,template-,keyed-,nested-conditional-}slot-rfor`) — Vue emits a correct dynamic named slot while React/Solid/Svelte/Lit emit a default-slot `{name: x}` scope param. Those four are the red-first test and the entire audited blast radius of reserving `:name` (repo-wide grep returns 5 `.rozie` hits; the fifth is `:name` on an `<input>` in Flatpickr). Design decisions locked: static-prefix family inference for consumer↔producer matching, `matchedFamily` as the per-target record-path dispatch switch, and the legacy Lit `data-rozie-params`/`observeRozieSlotCtx` path kept as fallback (no `@rozie/runtime-lit` major).
- **Phase 78 COMPLETE (2026-08-15)** — 13 commits, UNPUSHED. Shipped: 123 token declarations reconciled into 17 `base.css` files (values copied verbatim from each token's own inline `var()` fallback; `command-palette` 75, `date-picker` 25, `combobox` 14, `rete` 3, `slider` 2, `toast` 2, `resizable` 1, `data-table` 1), 6 dead `command-palette` `option-*` tokens removed across 4 theme files, new `docs/scripts/gen-theming-pages.mjs` + shared `docs/scripts/display-name.mjs`, 17 generated `docs/components/<slug>-theming.md` pages, 17 sidebar entries, and 16 hand-authored `## Theming` sections trimmed to intro+example+link-out (−241/+32). **Gate evidence:** cold turbo build 242/242, typecheck 311/311, test 137/137 (all 0 cached), dist-parity 1049/1049 zero drift, docs test 29/29, docs build 91.5s with check-anchors 268/0-broken, and a full-matrix Linux Docker VR run at **2176 passed / 0 failed / 7 known-class timing flakes** against existing baselines with NO `--update` — `git status --porcelain tests/visual-regression/__screenshots__/` empty, which is what actually proves render-neutrality. **Three conflicting-fallback tokens needed the CSS-wide `initial` keyword** (`combobox input-underline` reads with different fallbacks at its resting vs `:focus` sites; `slider fill-start`/`fill-end` are runtime-computed) — `initial` sets the guaranteed-invalid value so each `var()` keeps its OWN local fallback; declaring any single value would collapse the state. `data-table max-height` had no fallback to copy (`var(--x)` with no 2nd arg) → declared `none`, which is `max-height`'s CSS initial value and therefore the same computed result under invalid-at-computed-value-time. **Post-sign-off follow-up delivered in-phase:** heading quality was worse than recorded — 13 of 17 pages carried a paragraph-length heading, and `popover` (whose `base.css` has NO group comments, only a file-head doc block) headed its only table with that whole doc block. Root cause: `base.css` comments were never written to be headings and position alone can't separate a label from a note. Fixed by resolving each group from the whole preceding comment run — last LABEL (single-line, ≤72 chars after trimming box-drawing decoration) becomes the heading, other comments render as PROSE above the table, file-head doc blocks dropped, groups keyed by position so headingless groups don't collide. **Contract relaxed with Dan's approval: "every heading is a verbatim base.css comment" → "…a verbatim base.css LABEL".** Debt/notes: `REQ-78-*` IDs don't exist in REQUIREMENTS.md (registry uses `PARSE-01`/`SEM-01`/`ANGULAR-01` domain scheme; phases 76/77 also skipped it — pre-existing drift, no entries invented); `state.advance-plan` can't parse this 691KB STATE.md; wave 1 ran SEQUENTIALLY because worktrees are off here and 3 executors would race on `.git/index.lock`. **New coupling to remember: `base.css` comments are now rendered markdown** — a comment naming a tag (`<optgroup>`, `<div>`, `<Combobox>`) broke the VitePress build until `escapeAngles()` was added. See [[project_data_table_dedicated_docs_section]], [[project_docs_build_oom]], [[feedback_batch_vr_across_quick_series]].
- Phase 78 added (2026-08-15): Per-family token theming pages — a dedicated, GENERATED `docs/components/<slug>-theming.md` per token-shipping family, whose centrepiece is the complete `--rozie-*` customizable-token table. Brainstorm-approved with a design spec at `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-15-component-token-theming-pages-design.md` (gitignored per `commit_docs: false`) the planner should CONSUME (`--skip-research` candidate). Origin: Dan's ask for "a documentation page for each of our headless components that focuses on the customizable tokens." Scope grew from 13 headless → **all 17 families that ship `src/themes/`** (adds `data-table`, `embla`, `popover`, `rete`) on Dan's call. **The finding that shaped the design:** a probe deriving each family's public prefix from `base.css` and cross-referencing every literal `var(<prefix>…)` read under `src/` found **123 tokens the components read but `base.css` never declares** — `command-palette` 75, `date-picker` 25, `combobox` 14, `rete` 3, `slider` 2, `toast` 2, `resizable` 1, `data-table` 1 — so every family's "the full token vocabulary is in `themes/base.css`" claim is false for 8 of them, and hand-authoring a table would transcribe known-wrong data. Hence Dan chose **reconcile `base.css` FIRST, then generate**. Five steps: (1) reconcile all 17 `base.css` files, CSS-only, values copied verbatim from each token's existing inline fallback so rendering is byte-identical — `command-palette` gets its own task (75 of 123, with nested-fallback chains like `hotkey-bg`→`actions-hint-bg` where a mechanical copy could shift resolution); (2) new `docs/scripts/gen-theming-pages.mjs` wired into the `gen:usage`→`dev`/`build` chain, deriving the public prefix as the longest common segment-prefix of `base.css`'s `--rozie-*` declarations (**the prefix is NOT slug-derived**: `date-picker`→`--rozie-datepicker-`, `rete`→`--rozie-flow-`), token set = union of declarations and source reads; (3) 17 generated pages with `Token | Default` tables grouped by `base.css`'s own `/* … */` comments, replacing the hand-authored `data-table-theming.md`; (4) trim each `<slug>.md` `## Theming` to intro+example+link-out; (5) sidebar nav + `relatedLinks()` registration. Locked decisions: generated-not-hand-authored; all 17 families; no description column (names + group comments carry it — 450+ descriptions rejected as a liability); **no hard drift guard** (non-fatal `console.warn` only, promote later if reconciliation regresses); `surface_hash` untouched (it hashes the IR surface, not CSS). Generator gotcha to preserve: `.rozie` files contain PROSE like `` `var(--rozie-switch-*, <fallback>)` `` in comments (`switch/src/Switch.rozie:191`), so the read-scan regex must require a real segment and a trailing `,`/`)` or it captures bogus bare-prefix tokens. Gates: per-family `@rozie-ui/<slug>` build for the 8 touched families, ONE batched `vr.sh` union run over those 8 (the byte-identical-render claim is what actually needs proving), `@rozie/docs test` (surface_hash green), and `NODE_OPTIONS=--max-old-space-size=6144 @rozie/docs build` (runs `check-anchors.mjs` over the new links). NEXT: `/gsd-plan-phase 78`. See [[project_data_table_dedicated_docs_section]], [[project_comparison_page_surface_hash_guard]], [[project_docs_build_oom]], [[feedback_batch_vr_across_quick_series]].
- Phase 77 added (2026-08-04): `r-keynav` grid focus-model + multi-group — closes the gap cluster filed 2026-08-02 in the emitter-hardening backlog (Tier-3 CANDIDATE, date-picker pre-release audit): the Phase-71 primitive is strictly 1D (`orientation:'both'` = both arrow pairs → ±1; no row stride / PageUp-PageDown / row-wise Home-End / boundary→paging hook), ROZ986 forbids multiple groups per component, and disabled handling is skip-only (no APG focusable-but-inert). Brainstorm-approved with design contract `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-04-r-keynav-grid-design.md` (gitignored local artifact; copy into phase dir as SPEC.md — planner should CONSUME, `--skip-research` candidate). Dan locked 4 decisions (all recommended options): scope = grid cluster + multi-group with date-picker dogfood (1D family retrofits = fast-follow); grid syntax = `.grid(cols)` parameterized modifier (peggy args grammar; grid ⊥ focus-model; `.grid`+orientation and `.grid`+`.loop` = new ROZ993/994; bad cols = ROZ995); paging = `@keynav-page` event `{direction, reason:'pageup'|'pagedown'|'boundary', axis}` with machine-never-lands (author advances dataset + sets active model, P71 §6 ownership preserved); disabled = inert-by-default in grid mode (`.skipdisabled` opts back; 1D untouched). Multi-group = containment-scoped (nearest ancestor root; no naming syntax; ROZ986 retired; nested roots = ROZ996; ROZ984 reworded to ancestor-containment). Slices: keynav-core additive minor (grid branch + `page()` host hook + `KeynavPageDetail`) → compiler (bespoke `resolveKeynavModifiers` `.grid(expr)` + per-root `resolveKeynavGroups`) → six target pairs (thread grid config + `@keynav-page` like `@keynav-commit`) → fresh KeynavGridDemo 6-target behavior+VR gates → date-picker retrofit (day grid + drills onto r-keynav, delete hand-rolled roving/scheduleFocus from quick 260802-hla; its 24 VR cells + buildMonthGrid unit tests stay green UNMODIFIED as the regression net; single deliberate per-family rebless). Highest-risk seam (spec §10): each target controller must focus AFTER DOM update when active changes in the same tick as a month swap — red-first before deleting `scheduleFocus`. Additive-invariant until the retrofit wave (existing emit byte-identical). Depends on Phase 71 (complete). PLANNED 2026-08-04 same day: 9 plans (77-01…77-09, strictly sequential waves, planner=opus), plan-checker PASSED first iteration (all KNG-01…09 covered, clean linear dependency chain, red-first at every seam, no scope-reduction language). Planner surfaced 3 SPEC gaps + 2 code-verified corrections, all recorded as SPEC §10.5 amendments — headline: Gap-C association rule (strict nearest-ancestor containment would ROZ984-break every activedescendant/void-element root like the combobox `<input>`; final rule = single-root keeps P71 component-membership association verbatim, 2+ roots use strict containment), peggy Arg extension for `$`-dotted paths (`.grid($data.cols)` was unparseable), optional `index` on r-keynav-item (nested-loop flat-index), both drills verified `.grid(3)` (shared DRILL_COLS=3), day-panel wrapper added pixel-neutral-first. - Phase 75 added (2026-07-13): Cross-package published-leaf composition (Option A) — graduates cross-family composition past Phase 999.4's Option B (authoring-time source vendoring) to real composition of an already-*published* `@rozie-ui` leaf. Today `<components>` resolves exactly one local `.rozie` sibling path, emitted identically to all six targets via extension-swap; a published `@rozie-ui` leaf has a DIFFERENT package name per target (`@rozie-ui/listbox-react` vs `-vue` vs `-svelte` …), so there is no resolution path from a `<components>` entry to a published cross-framework package, no way to know the compiled child's slot/prop contract at compile time when its IR isn't in the same compiler pass (999.4 flagged this needs a generated manifest), and no story for threading slots through a compiled (non-`.rozie`) child. 999.4 explicitly rejected this as the biggest compiler change and deferred it pending "consumer install-size telemetry complaints about duplicated primitive bytes" (command-palette vendors listbox today and pays the duplicate-bytes cost per install). Dan chose to pick this up directly (2026-07-13) rather than wait for that trigger. Depends on Phase 999.4 (composition machinery + command-palette/listbox precedent, complete), Phase 66 (composed-component ref→Handle typing ×6, complete — Option A was explicitly deferred there too), the LOCKED `@rozie-ui` per-framework distribution model (`project_rozie_ui_distribution_model`). Cross-refs: Phase 999.5 gap-audit finding #2 (also names this open), memory `project_cross_family_components_composition_gap`. NEXT: `/gsd-discuss-phase 75`.
- Phase 74 added (2026-07-13): FlowCanvas Background variant + NodeResizer — closes the last 2 real React-Flow-parity gaps for `@rozie-ui/rete`, surfaced by the Phase 999.5 comparison-page audit and then re-verified against current source (the audit itself was reading a stale `rete-comparison.md`: "custom edge rendering bezier-only" and "NodeToolbar deferred" both turned out to already be shipped in Phase 44 — 44-02 edge types, 44-06 NodeToolbar — just never reflected in the doc). Confirmed by direct source read (`packages/ui/rete/src/FlowCanvas.rozie`): the canvas background is one fixed CSS dot-grid (`.rozie-flow-canvas` radial-gradient, color/size CSS-var-tunable but no variant switch, no lines/cross pattern, no way to turn it off via a prop) — no React-Flow-style `<Background variant="dots|lines|cross">` equivalent exists; and there is no node-resize affordance at all (grep for resize/NodeResizer turns up nothing node-related). Scope: (1) a background-variant prop (dots/lines/cross/none, config-prop-first per the established MapLibre stance cited in the doc) and (2) NodeResizer drag handles on the node-type-template render pipe, reusing the controlled-graph write-back discipline the whole Rete family already follows (fresh top-level graph object per resize, echo-guarded) — design specifics (handle placement, min/max size, aspect-lock, which corners/edges) to be locked via `/gsd-discuss-phase 74`, matching how every prior Rete phase (42/43/44) locked its design in a `-CONTEXT.md` before planning. Also includes: retiring the 2 stale "deferred" doc claims described above, and updating the comparison-matrix cells accordingly. Depends on Phase 44 (controlled-graph write-back, `<NodeType>`/`<Port>` registry, MEASURED node sizes already computed for MiniMap that NodeResizer's handle positioning can likely reuse) + Phase 999.5 (source of the corrected gap list). NEXT: `/gsd-discuss-phase 74`.
- Phase 71 added (2026-07-02): `r-keynav` — compiler-owned keyboard-navigation primitive. A core language/compiler-primitive phase (peer of Phase 33 reactive portal slots + Phase 36 context primitive), NOT a component-family phase. Ships the `r-keynav` directive family as a first-class author-side `.rozie` primitive that compiles to idiomatic native keyboard-list-navigation wiring ×6 (arrow/Home/End/typeahead + active-index + aria-activedescendant/roving-tabindex/id wiring + scroll-into-view). Surface: `r-keynav:<focus-model>.<modifiers>` (`activedescendant`|`tabindex`; `.vertical`/`.horizontal`/`.both`/`.loop`/`.typeahead`/`.skipdisabled`) + `r-keynav-item="{ label, disabled }"` + optional `r-keynav-active-class` + `@keynav-commit`. Brainstorm-approved with a committed design contract (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-02-keyboard-nav-primitive-design.md`, git `8b67b10f`/`bc2416df`) the planner should CONSUME (candidate `--skip-research`). Locked design decisions: data-driven item model with DOM-driven sugar (`:source` synthesized from the co-located `r-for`); association by shared state not DOM containment (one nav group per component in v1); primitive owns *active* (index/focus/aria) while the author owns *selection* (via `@keynav-commit`); hybrid emission (compiler wiring + per-target `@rozie/runtime-*` controllers `useKeynav`/`createKeynav`/`KeynavController`, mirroring the `.outside` precedent); decoupled from virtualization via an optional `KeynavWindower` contract that today's `@rozie-ui/headless-core` windowing already satisfies. Motivation: keyboard nav is hand-rolled in ~23 of 28 families + focus mgmt in ~20; `headless-core` dedupes the behavior but is component-library runtime code, not a compiler-owned primitive — no other framework (Radix/React-Aria/Ark) ships this as a declarative template directive compiling ×6 from one source. Scope v1 = roving list-navigation only; DEFERRED (out of scope): focus-trap/restore, standalone `$id` primitive, multiple nav groups per component, virtualization-as-a-compiler-primitive. Additive to the compiler (new directives no shipped `.rozie` uses → no corpus rebless expected). One open scope call for the planner: whether the flagship listbox retrofit is in-phase (carries a per-family rebless) or a fast-follow. House rules: red-first per emitter seam (`feedback_emitter_seam_surgical_per_seam`), behavior tests not just snapshots (`feedback_snapshot_tests_cement_bugs`), Linux VR baselines, validate via `turbo` not `pnpm -r`, no auto-push. Depends on Phase 70 + the `.outside` runtime-helper precedent + `@rozie-ui/headless-core` windowing. NEXT: /gsd-plan-phase 71 (clear context first). See [[project_next_port_wavesurfer]] (the session that surfaced the author-side-API moat), [[project_phase64_headless_windowing_listcore]].
- Phase 70 added (2026-07-01): DatePicker navigation & ergonomics features — four additive, in-scope enhancements to the shipped headless `@rozie-ui/date-picker` family (×6), brainstorm-approved with design spec copied into the phase dir as `SPEC.md` (source `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-01-datepicker-navigation-features-design.md`). Dan selected all four via AskUserQuestion: (1) **month/year quick-jump** via drill-in grids (day→month→year), interaction model = drill-in grids (over dropdown-caption/both), `monthYearNav` prop **DEFAULT-ON** (Dan chose default-on where standard UX over opt-in byte-stable) — heading becomes a `<button>`, escape hatch `:month-year-nav="false"`, so default output changes → Linux VR baselines regenerate; (2) **multiple months side-by-side** `numberOfMonths` (default 1 = byte-identical single-month, no extra wrapper; window slides by 1 month not by page); (3) **Today/Clear footer** `showFooter` (default off) + `#footer` slot `{ today, clear, todayIso }`, Today SELECTS today in single mode / navigates in range (Dan's choice over navigate-only); (4) **predicate/weekday disabling** — BOTH `disabledDaysOfWeek: Number[]` (serializable, plain attr ×6) AND `isDateDisabled: (iso)=>boolean` (Lit property-binding caveat like `disabledDates`/`presetRanges`), funneled through the single `isDayDisabled` gate. New pure helpers `buildMonthList`/`buildYearGrid` in `internal/buildMonthGrid.ts`, unit-tested in isolation; `grid()`→`grids()` for multi-month; drill grids reuse roving-tabindex + `[data-month]`/`[data-year]` querySelector walk. Stays inside deliberate non-goals (NO time-of-day / arbitrary multi-date / popover-input combo / non-Gregorian). House rules: red-first for new behavior, 6-target snapshots + Linux VR baselines refreshed, dist-parity rebless if `targets/*` touched, validate via `turbo` not `pnpm -r`, playground family entries.
