- Phase 66 added (2026-07-01): Composed-component ref → Handle typing across all 6 targets (Bundle C / Item 1). Closes the "refs-lowering type gap" from the cross-family composition backlog (Phase 999.4 / `project_cross_family_components_composition_gap`). A `<components>`-composed child referenced via `$refs.X` must type as the child's `$expose`-synthesized `<Name>Handle` uniformly ×6 so `$refs.child.method()` typechecks. Verified current-source (2026-07-01, read-only Explore): **Angular alone** is correct (`viewChild<ComponentType>` via `collectComponentRefTypes`/`componentRefs.ts:33` matching `RefDecl.elementTag` vs `ir.components[].localName`); **React/Vue/Svelte/Solid/Lit all fall back to `HTMLElement`** — their ref-typing switches key only on `RefDecl.elementTag` DOM cases, never consult `ir.components` nor `synthesizeHandleType.ts`. (Corrects the 4-day-old memory which said "solid = Handle" — Solid is `HTMLElement | null`.) `RefDecl` (`packages/core/src/ir/types.ts:290`) has no `isComponent`/`handleType` field. The one real fixture, command-palette (composes Combobox), works around it with a Lit shadow-pierce DOM query (`CommandPalette.rozie:249-256`, in-source comment attributing the gap). LOCKED: bring the 5 broken targets in line with Angular's component-instance approach, prefer a shared core resolver over 6 ad-hoc copies; retire the command-palette Lit shadow-pierce for a real `$refs.combobox.focusControl()` call (the end-to-end reward); DEFER Option A (per-target published-package resolution); byte-identity carve-out gated (command-palette re-vendor / A==B re-verified — only its emitted body should change). Sequence: Bundle C Item 4 (done, `846b5e59`) → Item 2 = Phase 65 (done) → **Item 1 = Phase 66 (this)** → Item 3 = Phase 67 (Vue family-child emitter). Verified file:line map staged in session scratchpad `phase66-refs-handle-findings.md`. Depends on Phase 999.4 + Phase 21 (`$expose`/`synthesizeHandleType.ts`) + Angular `componentRefs.ts` as reference impl. NEXT: /gsd-plan-phase 66.
- Phase 64 added (2026-06-27): Headless windowing + listCore extraction. Stands up a new source-only `@rozie-ui/headless-core` package holding two shared `.rzts` script-partials — a generic `windowing.rzts` (TanStack `virtual-core` math lifted from data-table's `virtualization.rzts` behind a no-op pin-extension seam so B13 parity holds byte-for-byte) and a focus-/input-mode-parameterized `listCore.rzts` (collection model + arrow/home/end/enter reducer + type-ahead + single/multi selection + filter). Born from Dan's prompt to lift data-table's only-virtualization primitive out for reuse AND collapse the listbox/combobox duplicated list logic. **Brainstorm-approved (2026-06-27); design doc `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-27-headless-windowing-listcore-design.md`.** Load-bearing question RESOLVED via 3 parallel read-only Explore agents: cross-package `.rzts` import works ×6 because `.rzts` are compile-time script-partials (Phase 54 `inlineScriptPartials` + `ProducerResolver`/enhanced-resolve which lists `.rzts` ahead of `.ts` and honors `exports`), dissolving into leaves with ZERO runtime dep → a shared package is a devDependency. Three decisions LOCKED via AskUserQuestion: (1) new `@rozie-ui/headless-core` source-only pkg (not co-located/relative, not data-table-as-home); (2) keep TanStack `virtual-core` (one primitive, not a forked dependency-free math core); (3) RETIRE Listbox's combobox/filterable mode + migrate command-palette to vendor the Combobox family — discovered mid-brainstorm that command-palette DEPENDS on `<Listbox combobox filterable=false>` as its search surface (not just overlap), so the migration is in-scope. listCore parameterized for activedescendant|roving but both families stay activedescendant today (no focus rewrite forced). Design sequencing = P0 prove the cross-package boundary ×6 with a smoke partial + VR cross-tree registration (`resolveCrossTreeBareImports`/`prebuildExtraRoots`/`tsconfig.app.json`) BEFORE moving real code → P1 extract `windowing.rzts` (data-table first consumer; `data-table-virtual` + `data-table-grid-virtual-parity` specs are the green gate; target zero emitter change) → P2 extract `listCore.rzts` (Listbox) → P3 Combobox owns type-to-filter + retire Listbox combobox + migrate command-palette ×6 → P4 windowed long-list demo + red-first specs ×6 (budget Solid rAF-defer per Phase-63 `parseInlineStyle`/grouping-settling hazards). House rules: red-first for new behavior, refactors gated by existing specs staying green, rebless dist-parity + target-* if any `targets/*` touched, validate via `turbo` not `pnpm -r`, Linux VR baselines. Depends on Phase 63 (stabilized `virtualization.rzts`/B13) + the shipped listbox/combobox/command-palette families + Phase 54 `.rzts` inline arch. NEXT: /gsd-plan-phase 64 (clear context first; design doc stands in for research — `--skip-research` candidate).
- Phase 63 added (2026-06-26): `@rozie-ui/data-table` grid mode — correctness hardening + full behavioral test battery. Kicked off by Dan ("grid is the pinnacle of our components and it needs a full battery of tests to prove it does what we think it does"). Approach = code audit FIRST (4 parallel read-only agents: focus/nav, range/clipboard/fill, edit-lifecycle, data-model integration) → fix found bugs → build the behavioral battery. Audit surfaced **26 correctness bugs** (12 P1 data-loss/keyboard-trap + 14 P2), each verified against `.rzts`/`.rozie` source AND Lit+Solid compiled output (shared-logic bugs, NOT emitter divergence). Consolidated working doc staged at `63-AUDIT.md` (B1–B27 with file:line + repro + caught-by-tests). Worst P1s: B1 edit click-away wedges grid (nav frozen), B7 fill-drag clobbers multi-column data, B11 Ctrl+C/V on active header = silent body mutation, B6 empty/all-filtered grid keyboard-unreachable. Four feature expansions LOCKED via AskUserQuestion + library research (focused-cell index conventions across AG Grid/Glide/react-data-grid/SlickGrid/Handsontable): **C1** unify focus index to ABSOLUTE filtered+sorted (`getPrePaginationRowModel`) in paginated+virtual + `getRowIndexRelativeToPage()` + `aria-rowindex=abs+1` (reverses today's page-relative-when-paginated; Dan initially leaned viewport-relative but research showed it's impossible for off-window `focusCell` + minority convention); **B13** full virtual↔grouping/expand/`#detail` render parity in the windowed `<tbody>`; **C2** treegrid row semantics (land on group rows + `aria-expanded`); **C3** range-tiling paste + Cut. House rules in force: red-first (no cementing bugs in snapshots — `feedback_snapshot_tests_cement_bugs`), surgical per-seam emitter fixes + dist-parity/VR rebless, validate via `turbo` not `pnpm -r`, Linux VR baselines. - Phase 58 added (2026-06-23): First-class prop documentation — an optional `docs: { description, deprecated, example }` object per prop in the `<props>` block, lowered to `ir.props[i].docs` (single source of truth) and surfaced TWO ways from that one field: (1) JSDoc on every target's synthesized prop type via a shared `buildPropJsdoc()` in `@rozie/core` (React interface / Vue `defineProps<T>` / Svelte `$props` / Angular `@Input` / Solid props / Lit `@property`) so consumers get IntelliSense prose ×6; (2) the docs props-table Description column (`renderReadme` / `validateDocsPropsTable` already iterate `ir.props` but fill it blank today). Mapping: `description`→leading JSDoc, `deprecated` (`true`→bare `@deprecated`, string→`@deprecated <msg>`), `example`→`@example`. Guardrails: whitelist `docs` keys with a NEW ROZ diagnostic on unknown/mistyped keys; strip `docs` from runtime prop-options emit; INERT-WHEN-ABSENT (no `docs` → no JSDoc → zero dist-parity/target-`*` snapshot churn ×6) so the capability ships cold and families opt in one at a time. PILOT: `@rozie-ui/data-table` `.rozie` props get `docs:` → regen 6 leaves + READMEs + props table (the only intended churn). - Phase 45 added (2026-06-13): `$clone` sigil — cross-target safe deep clone for reactive objects. The ONE emitter-addressable item from the Phase 44 close-out gotcha review (the other three — rete connection-signal scoping/ordering, the transient empty-connections frame, and the `@emit`-payload-vs-`e.detail` convention — are third-party-engine-behavior or already-consistent, not compiler-addressable). Verified during the review that the existing `$snapshot` sigil does NOT solve it — it lowers to identity on 5/6 targets (`$state.snapshot` only on Svelte), i.e. an unwrap, not an independent deep copy; FlowCanvas hand-rolled `cloneGraph` (JSON) for exactly this reason. Scope: register `$clone` (bare-sigil validator + reactivity dep-graph + core lowering hook); per-target proxy-safe deep-clone lowering in all 6 emitters (strategy per-target = planning decision D-01: JSON round-trip / `structuredClone`-over-`toRaw` / `$state.snapshot`); a lowering-time ROZ diagnostic flagging `structuredClone(<reactive binding>)` → `$clone` (D-02, ROZ123/127/128 guard pattern, conservative syntactic detection OK for v1); dogfood by retrofitting FlowCanvas `cloneGraph`→`$clone`; dist-parity rebless (emitter change) + full 6-target gate. - Phase 34 added (2026-06-06): CSS engine-DOM escape hatch — a core-compiler capability so a component `<style>` block can style ENGINE-RENDERED runtime DOM (ProseMirror `.is-editor-empty`, CodeMirror `.cm-*`) that never carries Rozie's `[data-rozie-s-*]` scope attr, working on all 6 targets incl. Lit shadow DOM. Discovered closing TipTap G3 placeholder: `:root { .sel {} }` nesting double-collects (React drops the global; others emit a dead scoped copy + the real one); `:global()` is dead on plain-CSS targets; the load-bearing complication is Lit — `rootRules` go to the document head (correct for inheriting custom props, WRONG for a selector rule that must reach shadow-mounted engine DOM). Open design decision: document-global (`:root`) vs component-own-scope-unscoped (`:deep`-like). Reusable across all engine-wrapper ports; prerequisite for a follow-on TipTap gap-closure phase (G3 placeholder CSS + G2 bubble/floating-menu slots). TipTap G3 behavior wiring (Placeholder ext → `placeholder` prop) already prototyped + reverted to clean tree pending this hatch. Decided with Dan inline 2026-06-06 (chose "scope as a GSD phase" over inline surgery on the CSS-scoping subsystem).

- [Phase 07.3.1]: Plan 02 — Blocker #2 (D-02) closed: Svelte producer emits object-shape snippet args (`{@render header({ close })}`); Snippet type tuple tightened from multi-arg to single-tuple object literal; 5 per-package snap fixtures re-blessed.
- [Phase 07.3.1]: Plan 03 — Blocker #3 (D-03) closed (partial — see Plans 05/07/08): Lit late-binding wrap `(e) => (this._<X>Ctx?.<param>)?.(e)` on click handlers + `_slotCtxWired_<name>` flag + microtask retry in firstUpdated + updated() re-attempt + disconnectedCallback flag reset.
- [Phase 07.3.1]: Plan 04 — D-04 dogfood acceptance gate satisfied: 6/6 modal-consumer-close.spec.ts cells GREEN; svelte+lit un-fixme'd in TARGETS_WHERE_CLOSE_PROPAGATES; dist-parity 336/336 GREEN; human-verified Svelte + Lit demos close-button on first paint.
- [Phase 07.3.1]: Plan 05 — D-LIT-15 closed: Lit producer pre-seeds `_hasSlot<X>` from light DOM in connectedCallback BEFORE super.connectedCallback() (`Array.from(this.children).some(el => el.getAttribute('slot') === '<X>')`). Closes chicken-and-egg deadlock where conditionally-rendered slot wrappers prevented the slot element from existing, which prevented queryAssignedElements from observing fills, which kept the slot wrapper hidden forever.
- [Phase 07.3.1]: Plan 06 — D-SV-16 closed: Svelte producer accepts a `snippets?: Record<string, Snippet<[any]>>` prop and resolves each named-snippet field via `header = $derived(__headerProp ?? snippets?.header)`. Closes the dynamic-name slot bridge gap where consumer-side `<template #[$data.slotName]>` emitted a `snippets={{ [name]: ... }}` prop that the producer never read.
- [Phase 07.3.1]: Plan 07 — D-LIT-17 closed: Lit consumer translates function-typed scoped-slot params (exact shape `this._<X>Ctx?.<param>`) to `(e) => (e.currentTarget as HTMLElement).dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('rozie-<X>-<param>', { detail: e, bubbles: true, composed: true }))`. Producer's existing host-listener wiring (emitHostListenerWiring.ts) receives the events end-to-end. Critical fix: dispatch on e.currentTarget (the clicked button inside the producer's light DOM) NOT on `this` (which is the consumer — PARENT of the producer); bubbling propagates UP through the producer.
- [Phase 07.3.1]: Plan 08 — D-LIT-18 closed: Lit consumer slot fills with multiple top-level elements spread `slot="<X>"` across each top-level element instead of wrapping in a synthetic `<div slot="<X>">`. Preserves the producer's flex-parent/flex-child contract — consumer's h2 + button + producer's close-btn are siblings inside the producer's `<header>`.
- [Phase 07.3.1]: Cleanup — vestigial `<Counter />` removed from Modal.rozie source (all 6 demos); 4 import-sourcemap tests retargeted from Modal.rozie to ModalConsumer.rozie; tests/regressions + tests/slot-matrix baselines refreshed against current emit; lit-lint config disables wc/no-child-traversal-in-connectedcallback for the D-LIT-15 pre-seed pattern.

Fixes the current silent-drop bug (an unknown `r-model` modifier compiles with zero diagnostics, emitting a dead input). Has an unresolved central design question — how custom `r-model` modifiers fit the event-shaped `ModifierImpl` registry — flagged for a `/gsd-spec-phase 12` → `/gsd-discuss-phase 12` round before planning. - Phase 13 added (2026-05-22): `$classSelector` author-side helper — a cross-target class-name-to-selector bridge for engine wrappers. New `$`-sigil helper (family of `$snapshot`/`$emit`/`$portals`) that the compiler lowers per-target: compile-time literal `".grip"` for Vue/Svelte/Solid/Angular/Lit, runtime `"." + styles.grip` lookup for React (CSS-Modules class hashing). Closes Bug 3 of the SortableList drag-desync debug session — React's class hashing breaks `handle=".grip"` passed literally into SortableJS, so React can't drag. Technically independent of Phases 9–12. Flagged for `/gsd-spec-phase 13` before planning to lock 7 open design questions.
- Phase 14 added (2026-05-22): Cross-framework attribute fallthrough — `r-bind="obj"` object-spread directive (object form only; no `r-bind:` colon syntax — `:attr` stays the single-named form), the `$attrs` accessor (consumer-passed attributes minus declared props), automatic root-element fallthrough with an `inheritAttrs`-style opt-out, per-target lowering (Lit + Angular are the hard targets — no native attr-object spread), and IntelliJ plugin + TextMate grammar sync. Surfaced when a user-test question ("what's the r-on syntax?") exposed that `r-on`/`r-bind` are listed in the TextMate grammar but never implemented in the compiler. Independent feature work, sequenced after the current backlog. Flag for `/gsd-spec-phase 14`: `inheritAttrs` flag spelling/location, class/style merge precedence vs explicit `:class`, whether `$attrs` includes listeners.
- Phase 15 added (2026-05-22): Cross-framework listener fallthrough — `r-on="obj"` object-spread directive (object form only; no `r-on:` colon syntax — `@event` stays the single-named form), the `$listeners` accessor (consumer-passed listeners — the consumer-side cluster, distinct from the author-side `<listeners>` block), per-target lowering incl. a runtime `click`→`onClick` listener-key adapter for React/Solid, reuse of Phase 14's fallthrough/`inheritAttrs` machinery. Folds in the IntelliJ plugin alignment (revert the wrong `r-on:` colon completion shipped in commit fd6b246, retext `r-on`/`r-bind` docs to object form, drop the `r-on:` modifier-parser branch) + TextMate grammar sync. Depends on Phase 14. Flag for `/gsd-spec-phase 15`: keep `$listeners` separate or merge into `$attrs` Vue-3-style.

### Phase 10 Decisions

- [Phase 10]: Plan 10-01: `sass` (dart-sass) declared as an optional peer dependency of `@rozie/core` (`peerDependenciesMeta.sass.optional: true`, `peerDependencies.sass: "^1.54.0"`) — a plain-CSS consumer never pulls it (SPEC-REQ-3); pinned `1.97.3` (no caret) devDependency in `@rozie/core` + `tests/dist-parity` for deterministic SCSS compile.
- [Phase 10]: Plan 10-01: peer floor is `^1.54.0` (RESEARCH Discretion #3 supersedes CONTEXT.md's `^1.45.0`) — the `charset` option, load-bearing for the dist-parity byte gate, ships since dart-sass 1.54.0.
- [Phase 10]: Plan 10-01: new `packages/core/src/parsers/resolveSass.ts` — synchronous `loadSass(): SassModule | null` mirrors the `solid-detect.ts` `createRequire(import.meta.url)` idiom; returns `null` only on `MODULE_NOT_FOUND` (so `parseStyle` can emit ROZ085), re-throws any other error; fully sync — no `await import()`, no public-API break. Re-exported from the `@rozie/core` barrel.
- [Phase 10]: Plan 10-02: `#block-style-scss` TextMate injection rule (D-09) — added to `tools/textmate/syntaxes/rozie.tmLanguage.json`, registered FIRST before `#block-style` in the top-level `patterns` array so first-match-wins routes `<style lang="scss">` to `source.css.scss`; modeled on the Phase 9 `#block-script-ts` precedent (`lang=` lookahead, single/double-quoted). Generic `#block-style` unchanged — plain `<style>`/`lang="css"` still route to `source.css`.
- [Phase 10]: Plan 10-02: playground SCSS grammar registration (D-10) — `examples/playground/src/textmate-setup.ts` imports `tm-grammars/grammars/scss.json?raw` (`tm-grammars@1.31.15` already a dependency, no new package) and adds `source.css.scss` to `GRAMMAR_JSON_BY_SCOPE`; NO `LANGUAGE_SCOPES` entry — SCSS only appears embedded inside `.rozie`. `tools/textmate/fixtures/CounterScss.rozie` is the highlight-verification fixture (NOT in dist-parity).
- [Phase 10]: Plan 10-04: `substituteCompiledStyle` (new `packages/core/src/codegen/`) splices `ast.style.cssText` into the body-only `ast.style.loc` span — Pattern 2; the six `emitStyle.ts` files stay byte-for-byte unmodified (SPEC-REQ-2). Called from `compile.ts` (CLI + babel-plugin inherit) and every per-target `@rozie/unplugin` pipeline.
- [Phase 10]: Plan 10-04: [Rule 1 fix] a length-changing splice invalidates `ast.blocks` — the unplugin `emitSourceAndBlocks` helper re-derives `blockOffsets` via `splitBlocks` on the substituted string when a substitution occurred; `compile.ts` is unaffected (never passes `blockOffsets`, emitters re-derive from `opts.source`). Surfaced as `MagicString.overwrite: end is out of bounds` on the 5 SCSS Leg-4 dist-parity cells.
- [Phase 10]: Plan 10-04: `PortalListStyledScss` is the 12th dist-parity example (single-file fork of `PortalListStyled`); 392/392 green across 12 examples × 6 targets × 4 entrypoints; the eleven plain-CSS baselines stayed byte-identical (SPEC-REQ-8 — `git diff` on fixtures empty); zero SCSS leakage in the 9 new baselines (SPEC-REQ-6).

- Plan 80-03 Task 1 probe: Angular JIT compilation (@angular/compiler's compileDirective/compileComponent, used by TestBed's platformBrowserDynamicTesting) does NOT support signal-based content/view queries (contentChildren/contentChild/viewChild/viewChildren). Verified via Angular 21.2.13 core source: directiveMetadata() derives ALL query metadata exclusively from ReflectionCapabilities.ownPropMetadata(type), which only reads LEGACY property-decorator metadata (@ContentChildren/@ViewChild). Signal query fields called as plain initializers (no decorator) are invisible to JIT — confirmed empty across 6+ independent repro configurations (Babel legacy decorators, Babel stage-3 decorators, tsc transpileModule x2, zero-transform hand-authored JS, explicit signals:true flag, multiple detectChanges/whenStable ticks). Legacy @ContentChildren on an IDENTICAL directive/template/TestBed setup returns the fill correctly. This blocks 80-03's rozieSlotDesign.probe.test.ts (Task 1) as specified — D-07's JIT-TestBed-under-Vitest harness design cannot validate contentChildren() the way it validates decorator-based queries. Escalated per plan's explicit 'STOP, do not work around, escalate' instruction. Candidate fixes identified but NOT implemented pending direction: (a) real AOT compilation via @angular/compiler-cli instead of JIT, (b) a test-only shim that synthesizes propDecorators-shaped metadata from signal-query field source text before JIT compiles (mirrors what ngtsc does natively; would not touch the actual RozieSlot/emitter design), (c) accept legacy-decorator-only runtime proof + non-runtime static verification for the signal form.

| 260830-j53 | **Lit and Angular dropped EVERY leading comment on a top-level declaration promoted into the component class — 1370 apiece.** Both lift members as hand-built STRINGS (`generate(decl)`/`renderExpression`/rebuilt arrow or `t.classMethod`; Angular `genCode(d.init)`/`genCode(d)`) and none carries the STATEMENT's comments. A per-branch rule is impossible: @babel/parser attaches a between-statements comment to BOTH neighbours, so whichever side a local rule picks, the other double-prints or drops. **Four properties, each found by MEASURING not reading:** (1) **look back** — claim the PREVIOUS statement's unclaimed trailing comments plus your own leading ones; inline that's one shared object, but across a `.rzts` boundary the spliced successor has nothing attached so prev-trailing is the ONLY copy (omit → partial-inline byte-identity guards go red); (2) **span the import block** — it's a separate printer, unseeded → **132 dupes (Lit) / 155 (Angular)**, but seeding from every comment merely ATTACHED to an import node over-corrected and **LOST 16**, so seed from what it ACTUALLY EMITTED; (3) **unclaim** when a statement produces no member (consumed by `$computed`/lifecycle/`$provide`) so its real printer still renders it — claiming without emitting silently DROPS comments, strictly worse than double-printing; (4) **final flush after the loop**. | 2026-08-30 | 7f752ef83 (lit), 7bbca75dd (angular), e203f0555 (baseline advance), + changeset | **Green — and the headline is a bug NO AUTOMATED GATE CAUGHT.** Property (4) was missing on Angular: pending comments attach on the NEXT iteration, so the LAST statement's were claimed then dropped — `examples/TodoList.rozie` emitted **ZERO** comments on Angular while Lit emitted the same block fine. target-angular **710/711**, dist-parity **1049/1049** and angular-runtime **196/196** were ALL GREEN with it live. Now three-way: **LOST / de-duped / DUPLICATED**. Final: **Lit 53 files +5311, Angular 52+ files +5266, ZERO lost, ZERO non-comment bytes changed on both**, plus 16 pre-existing double-prints fixed per target. | 260830-cfi | **Angular and Lit lowered a component-state write to `this.<name>` inside a non-arrow member of an object literal, where `this` is the OBJECT and not the component** — the write landed on the object, the component never updated, and nothing was reported. Silent, zero-diagnostic, 2/6 targets (React/Vue/Svelte/Solid close over the binding). `redirectNestedThis` exists to repair exactly this class, but its guard returned early for ANY top-level non-arrow function on the reasoning that top-level ⇒ promoted class method ⇒ `this` is the component. False for an object-literal member: a top-level `const api = { load() { … } }` is promoted to a class FIELD, so `api`'s members have no function parent and read as top-level, yet their `this` is `api`. **Scope was wider than the backlog recorded** — `project_emitter_redirect_nested_this_gap` described only "a `let` read via `this` inside a `.then()` nested in an object-literal method"; probing the shape space showed the nested callback is irrelevant and EVERY non-arrow object member is affected (method shorthand, getter, function-expression property), identically on both targets. The arrow-property form was always correct and is the reason the fix works: an arrow member of a class-field initializer already resolves `this` to the instance. Fix = detection (`isObjectMemberFunction` makes object-literal membership a non-component-`this` context) + host (when the outermost enclosing function IS the object member, no function can hold the alias, so the field initializer is wrapped in an arrow IIFE carrying it: `api = (() => { const __rozieSelf = this; return { … }; })();`). An object nested inside a function keeps the existing function host and emits no IIFE. IIFE chosen over rewriting methods to arrow properties because it preserves the object's own method semantics and is the ONE mechanism that also covers getters/setters, which cannot be arrow-converted. **One real deviation, caught by the existing suite rather than by inspection:** the unguarded fix silently deleted the entire reactive `effect(...)` bridge from every emitted `$provide` provider. `emitContext.bindProvidedValue` already owns that seam — it wraps the payload in a host-capturing IIFE, rewrites every `ThisExpression` to `__rozieCtxHost`, **and keys its reactivity detection on finding those `ThisExpression`s** — and `redirectNestedThis` runs earlier, so it consumed them first. Classified as ours by re-running with the fix stashed (9/9 green without it), then diagnosed by DIFFING the emitted provider, not by reading the pass. Ignoring it was worse than reported — the verb then did `connInstances.set(...)` and returned a truthy id, registering a **phantom edge the engine never took** (corrupting the next reconcile diff) while the `programmatic++` wrapper echo-suppressed the `connection-rejected` emit — invisible on all three channels at once. **The genuinely silent path was elsewhere:** `reconcileConnections`' three guards (`:2884` unknown node, `:2891-2892` missing port, `:2899` rejected add), where a typo'd socket key in a declarative `graph` prop yields no edge, no error, forever — the shape of the reporter's actual bug. Timer cancelled in the existing `$onMount` teardown; memo cleared in `clear()`. **D-04:** reconcile's rejected-add now rolls back the optimistic `connInstances`/`connMeta` seed. **D-05 held:** the `connection-rejected` emit contract is untouched — console-only channel; changing which paths emit that event is an observable contract change that gets its own decision, not a bug-fix rider. | 2026-08-27 | 5e2e6974b | **Green, verified at orchestrator level rather than taken on report.** `pnpm --filter @rozie-ui/rete build` re-run and the six leaves came back **byte-identical** (tree clean) — the real proof no leaf was hand-patched to satisfy a grep gate. ONE canvas-scope `ResizeObserver` over each node's `box` is the mechanism-correct trigger — "the box changed size", regardless of why — subsumes option 1, and closed finding 2 for free. | 260825-hp3 | **Collapsed the redundant whole-workspace builds that dominate CI compute — 16 full `pnpm turbo run build` invocations reduced to 6 via a per-workflow `build` producer job.** Baseline push `f699edef`: 306.6 CI min across 11 workflows / 24 jobs, with the build step measured at **10m58.99s** and **~86% of all CI time**. **The session brief's diagnosis was wrong in two load-bearing places and both were tested, not assumed.** (1) *"The turbo cache is broken / namespaced per job"* — it is not: normal push `7f11f88df` reported `Cached: 234 cached, 243 total` in `4m25.736s`; the `0 cached, 243 total` / `10m58.99s` on `f699edef` was **legitimate**, because ~70 commits including `packages/core` emitter changes had landed since that cache's source commit. (2) *"Release version bumps invalidate everything"* — bumped one leaf's version, re-ran `turbo run build --dry=json`, diffed all 520 task hashes: **4 changed, not 243**, with `hashOfInternalDependencies` and `hashOfExternalDependencies` byte-identical, and `f699edef` never touched `pnpm-lock.yaml`. (3) **Turborepo Remote Caching deliberately NOT added** — it needs a producer finishing before its consumers start, but all 24 jobs begin in the same second (`18:37:17Z`; React's 5 cells at `18:37:20Z`), so all 24 would miss simultaneously and build anyway: real work for ~zero minutes saved. Actual cause is **redundancy**, not cache misconfiguration. **The 260819-mqx no-gating rule is preserved, not reopened:** `vue-matrix.yml` documents a real incident where a `needs:` chain let a red upstream SKIP a downstream job and mask a genuine `vite build` failure for a whole backlog — so the producer asserts nothing, every consumer carries `if: ${{ !cancelled() }}` (a red producer does not skip it), and each consumer keeps its own `turbo run build` as a real fallback; worst case degrades to the old behaviour, never to a skip. That header comment was amended rather than left contradicting the code. Consumers are restore-only, which also fixes the observed `Unable to reserve cache … another job may be creating this cache` that 4 of the 5 React cells hit. **Scope corrected against the brief:** `docs.yml` excluded (already `--filter`ed + already `needs:`-chained, zero full builds), the three single-job workflows excluded (a producer would only ADD a job), and `angular-matrix`'s `integration-build` reverted to its original gating after the edit script touched it. Task 2 audited all **31** `no output files found` warnings — **all 31 safe**, no false-green: 28 `@rozie-ui/*` codegen packages (verified **29/29** carry a `@rozie/core` dep edge, so a core change invalidates them and codegen re-runs), 2 intentional `echo` no-ops, and `@rozie/docs` (whose site build bypasses turbo entirely in `docs.yml`); mechanism confirmed because `@rozie-ui/sortable-list` is the only codegen package absent from the warning list AND the only one with an explicit `"outputs": []` override. | 2026-08-25 | 12eb5eed9 | **VERIFIED IN CI — push `69040d2ba`, 9/9 green; consumer build 10m59s → `1.986s >>> FULL TURBO` (243/243 cached), zero cache-reserve collisions. Attribution here ≈ 50 min from the change, ~110 min confound; on a cold `packages/core` push the change is worth ~110 min because consumer cost is now cache-state independent.** All 14 workflows parse clean with **`uniqueKeys` enforced**, which caught and fixed a duplicate `needs:` key the edit script introduced in `angular-matrix.yml`. Structural assertions all pass: producer exists / saves exactly once / runs a full turbo build, and every chained consumer has the `!cancelled()` guard, uses no save-capable cache action, and retains its own `turbo run build` fallback; no stale `turbo-${{ github.job }}` keys remain in the six touched workflows. **Expected 306.6 → ~205 CI min (−33%), NOT the brief's implied ~5 h** — `docs.yml` was already optimal and four of the six workflows only had two builds to collapse. **Wall-clock gets ~1–2 min WORSE per workflow** because the producer serializes ahead of the cells; compute was the stated target and this is a deliberate trade, stated rather than hidden. **Nothing has run in CI** — that needs a push, withheld per `feedback_no_autopush`. | 260824-rhi | **Closed phase-82 code-review finding WR-01 — ROZ098/ROZ099 fired a factually FALSE message on an `r-match` root that has a real, unconditionally-rendered host element.** The gated-root check treated every `TemplateMatch` single root as element-less, but `<div r-match>` (unlike `<template r-match>`) carries a real wrapper in `TemplateMatchIR.hostElement` (`types.ts:1334`) that renders unconditionally — only the content nested inside it varies per branch. So the warning claimed "there is no unconditional element to attach the inherited attributes to" on a shape that has one, its hint ("move the gating condition onto a child so the root element is unconditional") was inapplicable because the root already was unconditional, and the spread that could have landed on the host did not. Same file already special-cased `hostElement` at line 158 for R9 detection — the gated-root check just never made the distinction. **Addressable set measured BEFORE scoping, as instructed:** 648 `.rozie` files repo-wide, 7 use `r-match`, **exactly one** has a root-level `r-match` with a hostElement (`examples/match/RealElementHost.rozie`); `tools/textmate/fixtures/RModelMatch.rozie` nests its `<div r-match>` under a root `<div class="rmm">` so it never reaches the single-structural-root check and is a highlighting fixture anyway; **zero shipped `@rozie-ui` leaves use an `r-match` root at all** — hence quick, not a phase. Fixed BOTH halves per the locked decision that the message alone is a half-measure: both hand-duplicated validators (D-17 — both moved or the listeners half stays broken) narrow to `TemplateMatch && !hostElement`, and both synthesizers resolve the host as the spread destination via a shared `resolveMatchHostRoot`. **`countRootElements` deliberately untouched** — it buckets a root `TemplateMatch` into `otherCount`, so a lone `<div r-match>` already returns 1 and `<div r-match>` + a sibling element already returns 2 (correctly; the host really IS a second root); leaving it alone is what keeps the T-82-06 predicate-agreement seam intact, and `resolveMatchHostRoot` mirrors `resolveSingleStructuralRoot` so it agrees with `countRootElements === 1` exactly. Deliberately NOT slot-tolerant (D-01): `<div r-match>` + `<slot/>` still counts 2 and still errors, unchanged. The new guards assert the IDE surface is a one-directional **superset** of core (knowing more stays legal for retired codes; knowing less never does), closing the one open cross-editor link — TextMate ⊇ `rozie-globals.d.ts`. **A behavioural assertion backs the set check** because set-membership alone cannot catch an alternation-ORDERING regression: every trailing group in the directive rule is optional, so `keynav` placed before `keynav-item` would still "contain" both while tokenising `r-keynav-item` as `r-keynav` + orphaned `-item`. New entries are ordered longest-first, mirroring the pre-existing `else-if | else` guard. **PHASE 2 — release prep (same session, Dan: "use the same publish rules as all the other packages... let's prepare to release it").** The extension had never been published (marketplace 404) and had no release path at all. Added one mirroring `release.yml` exactly: `.github/workflows/release-vscode.yml` (manual dispatch, `dry_run` **defaults true**, uploads a sideloadable `.vsix` on both paths, live run publishes that EXACT artifact via `--packagePath` so what was verified is what ships) and `scripts/release-precheck-vscode.mjs` / `pnpm release:precheck:vscode`, mirroring the npm precheck's (a)-(f) letters against the Marketplace with matching `--gate`/`--offline` modes and exit codes. **Two letters necessarily diverge, both structural:** (e) becomes "every declared runtime dep is actually INLINED into dist/extension.js" (since `vsce publish --no-dependencies` ships no node_modules), and (f) becomes server-bundle drift rather than tarball drift. **The (e) check false-failed on its first run** — the draft flagged any declared dependency and fired on `vscode-languageclient`, which is in fact fully bundled (0 bare requires; only node builtins + `vscode` external). | 260820-7hj | **REAL product bug fixed — command-palette's second Escape was silently dropped after a level pop.** Symptom: navigate into a palette level, press Escape twice to dismiss; ~1-in-3 (full VR matrix) / ~1-in-6 (isolated) the palette STAYS OPEN at root with the popped level's query restored. Live in EVERY published `@rozie-ui/command-palette` build, all six targets. **Mechanism (proven live, not inferred):** `goBack()` → `reopenComboboxPopup()` called `deepActiveElement().blur()` SYNCHRONOUSLY and only refocused inside `requestAnimationFrame`, parking focus on `<body>` for the gap. The Escape funnel is a BUBBLE-phase `@keydown="onPanelKeydown($event)"` on the frame div, so a keydown in that window never entered the subtree and `onPanelKeydown` NEVER RAN — captured via a `page.addInitScript` window-level probe: `defaultPrevented === false`, `composedPath() === [body, html, document, window]`. **Fix:** move focus TO `$refs.frame` (+ `tabindex="-1"`) instead of blurring to nothing — still fires the input's `@blur` (preserving the popup-reopen trigger) while keeping keydowns in the subtree. ONE change in shared `.rozie` source, all six targets. **Three wrong theories were falsified along the way, two of them mine:** (a) focus loss — WRONGLY ruled out because the captured DOM showed the combobox `[active]`, but that sample was taken AFTER the rAF refocus; an end-state snapshot cannot rule out a transient race; (b) the test's page-global `deepQuerySelectorAllCount` counting a sibling palette (the demo does host 3) — falsified, the counted option was INSIDE the open dialog; (c) a `resolveEscape` mis-route — falsified, the handler never ran at all. | 2026-08-20 | 66ce16500 (RED), 7f11f88df | **Deterministic gate, not a lucky pass:** the RED test forces the race with a holdable `requestAnimationFrame` via `page.addInitScript` (pattern from `lit-listener-teardown.spec.ts:67`), observed RED on all 6 targets across 3 consecutive runs pre-fix. Post-fix isolated `command-palette` runs went 96 → **102 passed** (the 6 new gate tests, one per target). **10/10 consecutive clean Docker VR runs** (at a 1-in-6 base rate, 10 clean alone would still be ~16% likely if unfixed — the red→green deterministic gate is the actual proof, the loop is corroboration). Full union VR `{"status":"passed","failedTests":[]}`. Orchestrator-verified independently: cold `build --force` produced **ZERO drift** (still exactly 13 files, ALL under `packages/ui/command-palette/` — emitter-blast-radius STOP condition never tripped), `turbo run test --force` **140/140**, `turbo run typecheck --force` **315/315**, tree clean. **Versions deliberately UNCHANGED** (react 0.4.6, lit/solid/vue 0.4.4, svelte 0.4.3, angular 0.4.2) — the leaves were bumped-but-unpublished because `release.yml` was never dispatched, so the fix ships under those same numbers; a new changeset would double-bump to 0.4.7 and strand the already-pushed changelog entries. A one-line fix entry was appended to each of the 6 existing CHANGELOG blocks instead. **This is why the release was held** — dispatching when first asked would have shipped this bug in 0.4.6 ×6 targets, after which a fresh patch wave would have been mandatory. | 260819-i06 | **Angular 21 matrix leg FIXED — and the two obvious fixes were both wrong, with evidence.** The demo typecheck failed 5x TS2739 on `TemplateRef`. Cause: **Angular 21 turned `TemplateRef` from an abstract, private-free class into a CONCRETE class with two `private` fields** (`_declarationLView`, `_declarationTContainer`), and TS compares privates NOMINALLY. `@rozie/runtime-angular` must carry an `@angular/core` devDependency to build via ng-packagr, so pnpm materialises a second instance under `packages/runtime/angular/node_modules/` — which is what its built `.d.ts` resolves to once tsc follows the workspace symlink realpath. Demo on 21, that one on 19, and `RozieSlot.templateRef` crosses the boundary in every slot-bearing component. **Fix: `baseUrl` + `paths` module-IDENTITY pin of `@angular/core` to each project's own copy** — same pattern and rationale as the `vite` pin in tests/angular-runtime/tsconfig.json. **Eliminated with evidence: (1) bumping runtime-angular to build against Angular 21 does NOT work — pnpm keys instances by PEER CONTEXT, and the store already holds TWO copies of the same version 21.2.13 (zone.js 0.14.10 vs 0.15.1); a probe assigning `TemplateRef` between two same-version instances still fails with 'separate declarations of a private property'. Same version != same instance. (2) The `TemplateRef<any>` vs `<unknown>` widening ng-packagr emits is a RED HERRING — private nominality fails either way.** The Angular 19 leg passes STRUCTURALLY, not by luck: v19's `TemplateRef` is abstract and private-free, so instance splits are invisible to it (verified by the same probe). **Not a shipped-package defect** — a published consumer resolves `@angular/core` as an unmet peer from its own tree, exactly one instance; the split is a workspace devDependency artifact, so pinning restores REPRESENTATIVE behaviour rather than masking a real incompatibility. Also caught a **second latent copy of the same bug**: `tests/integration/angular-analogjs` imports `RozieSlot` via `RecordPathProducer.rozie.ts` and CI overrides it to Angular 21 too — masked only because `integration-build` is `needs:` the job that was already failing. (The 19 leg also has an instance split — demo on zone.js@0.14.10 vs runtime-angular 0.15.1, both 19.2.22 — it just doesn't bite.) **Integration project at 21: the second pin was LOAD-BEARING** — `tsc --noEmit` without it FAILS with the same TS2739 at `RecordPathProducer.rozie.ts:34`; with it, clean; `vite build` + `ɵcmp` AOT grep clean. **NEW COVERAGE GAP FOUND: `tests/integration/angular-analogjs` has NO typecheck gate** — `integration-build` only runs `vite build` + a selector grep, and analogjs's build does NOT surface TS2739 (proven: built green WITH the known type error present). | 260711-cy1 | **Fixed a code-review finding on the R11-1 fix: React keyed loop (`r-for … :key`) with a mustache body silently dropped the `:key`.** After R11-1 made mustache-body loops render, a keyed one (`<slot r-for :key>` / interpolation / nested-conditional body) still emitted `{rows.map((row) => (…slot expr…))}` with NO `key=` — React's `emitLoop` sets `keyCode` as `childCtx.pendingKey`, consumed only by a child ELEMENT; a mustache has no element to carry it, and the single-child branch (unlike the multi-child `else`) never wrapped in a keyed Fragment. Result: React "unique key" warning + INDEX-based reconciliation → per-row DOM state (focus, scroll, uncontrolled inputs) mis-associates on reorder. Solid was already correct (`<Key … by={(row)=>row.id}>`). **Fix (React-only, +6 lines):** in `emitLoop`'s single-child branch, when the body is a mustache (`stripBalancedMustache(rendered) !== null`) AND `keyCode !== null`, register `Fragment` and emit `<Fragment key={keyCode}>${rendered}</Fragment>` (original `{…}` mustache as the JSX child) — mirroring the existing multi-child keyed-Fragment branch; keyless mustache bodies stay `(inner)`, element bodies unchanged. | 260701-eiw | **Fixed the Lit VR host Counter (and Modal/TodoList) being frozen — clicking `+` did nothing.** Root cause (proven via direct `createLitControllableProperty` reproduction): `entry.lit.ts` seeds model props via `Object.assign(el, DEFAULT_PROPS[example])`, hitting the emitted `set value(v){ notifyPropertyWrite(v) }` (changed from `write(v)` to fix a two-way SortableList desync — `emitScript.ts:347`), which UNCONDITIONALLY flips the standalone component into controlled mode. With no parent listener, the internal `write()` (Counter `increment`) only dispatches a `value-change` event and `read()` never updates → frozen (same strict-controllable behavior React/Solid have). React/Solid's `toUncontrolledProps` remap can't be reused — the Lit emitter bakes `defaultValue` into the controllable and emits no `default<Key>` prop, so remapping would drop meaningful seeds and change screenshots. Touching the runtime `notifyPropertyWrite` seed semantics was REJECTED (risks the container-gated nested-Kanban race). **Host-only fix:** `entry.lit.ts` wires a per-`MODEL_PROPS` `<prop>-change` writeback listener (`el[prop]=e.detail`) after non-demo mount so the host acts as the controlling parent — value round-trips through the setter, controlled latch updates, `read()` reflects; the helper's one-shot round-trip token prevents re-dispatch loops; seed unchanged so first paint/screenshots unaffected. Re-blessed the lone `.outside` svelte fixture (Dropdown.svelte.snap); restored the popover VR cell's direct-model-write step to the programmatic-OPEN ×6 proof (reverting the set-closed workaround) + regenerated popover/listbox svelte leaves. | 2026-06-24 | 66d1bb0e, 8fb624d8 | **Verified by orchestrator (independent re-run against a force-rebuilt toolchain)**: `turbo build --force` 205/205; svelte target tests 282/282 cold (reblessed snapshot matches emit); clean 6-target VR build; **36/36 `.outside`-family behavioral specs pass ×6** (popover restored programmatic-OPEN proof green on svelte + no regression in combobox/dialog/otp/toaster/switch). New non-optional `PropDecl.required: boolean`; `lowerProps` reads the `required:` key (literal `true` only). Settled semantic (discussed with Dan first, prose not menus): `required: true` is the **sole** determinant of requiredness, `default:` is orthogonal — three states (req+no-default ⇒ required; any `default:` ⇒ optional; neither ⇒ optional, `T\|undefined`), the Vue Options-API model. `required: true` + `default:` is incoherent → default dropped + new **ROZ014** warning. All six emitters now branch on `prop.required`: React/Solid non-optional interface field, Vue non-optional `defineProps` member + `defineModel(…,{required:true})`, Svelte non-optional `Props` member + bare/`$bindable()` destructure, Angular `input.required`/`model.required` **only** when flagged (behavior change — was emitted for every no-default prop), Lit `name!: T` for required + `name?: T` for optional (260521-mj9 prong-2 `!:` rebound from the custom-type heuristic to the `required` flag; fabricated builtin `=''`/`=0`/`=false` defaults removed for no-default props). Lit `model:true`+`required:true` keeps a fabricated attribute-backing initializer — accepted, documented Lit-ism. | 260526-no7 | Fix React `:deep()` CSS Modules class-hashing — surfaced when the user observed the Kanban demo's columns NOT laying out side-by-side on React in the playground compare view, despite the 260526-mk4 `:deep()` lift emitting structurally-correct `.board[data-rozie-s-92e6b8fa] .rozie-sortable-list { … }`. Root cause: React uses CSS Modules; the bundler hashes ALL class names in the emitted `.module.css`, so the lifted `.rozie-sortable-list` got module-hashed to a consumer-side hash that never matched the producer-side hash (SortableList renders `.rozie-sortable-list` from ITS own CSS Module). Fix: `hoistDeep()` in `packages/targets/react/src/emit/scopeCss.ts` now wraps the lifted compound in a single `:global(...)` postcss-selector-parser pseudo (combinators INSIDE the deep payload preserved INSIDE the wrap), and tags the wrapper itself in the `deepLifted` WeakSet so `appendScopeToOneCompound`'s scope-skip logic handles it unchanged. Solid not affected (runtime `__rozieInjectStyle` with literal class names — no CSS Modules pipeline); Svelte already wraps in `:global { ... }`; Vue/Angular handle natively; Lit shadow-DOM out of scope (documented limitation, `::part` is the future answer). (2) command-palette: `matchesActionKey` only ever parsed `$mod+<letter>`/bare-letter while `formatKeyToken` already rendered multi-modifier badges — a `$mod+$shift+k`-style actionKey badged but never fired; fixed via a shared `parseKeyToken` grammar both now consume (RED-first); also hoisted the per-row Apple-platform sniff to `$onMount` and collapsed 5 mechanically-identical per-level accessors into `currentFrameField`. Discovered + worked around (comment reword, logged to deferred-items.md) a real compiler gap: a `<script>`-literal INSIDE a `<data>` block comment silently corrupts the whole IR (emits/slots/expose → [], zero diagnostics) — the documented cross-tag-literal splitter gap, now reproduced concretely. (3) toast: per-row `depth()` was an O(n) `findIndex` scan × n rows = O(n²)/render; replaced with the `r-for` loop's own index (O(1), zero new state — sidesteps the `$computed` Solid-aliasing pitfall entirely). Added `scripts/check-changeset-private-packages.mjs` (+ `--self-test`) so a changeset naming a private family root (e.g. `@rozie-ui/toast`) fails loud instead of silently versioning nothing; wired into `package.json` + `release-precheck.mjs`'s structural audit path. Deduped ~20 byte-identical/near-identical shadow-piercing Playwright walkers across 3 VR spec files into `_shadow-utils.ts` (empirically verified the page.evaluate-closure-serialization constraint against a live headless Chromium page before committing); entangled walkers (geometry math, live-element interaction) left as-is + documented.
